//===== TERMUXFILE: config.py =====
import os
import random

# ── Discord ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
DISCORD_TOKEN = os.getenv("DISCORD_TOKEN", "YOUR_DISCORD_TOKEN_HERE")

# ── AI Provider ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
AI_PROVIDER = os.getenv("AI_PROVIDER", "openrouter")  # "groq" or "openrouter"

# ── API Key Rotation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
GROQ_KEYS = [
    k.strip()
    for k in os.getenv("GROQ_KEYS", os.getenv("AI_API_KEY", "")).split(",")
    if k.strip()
]
OPENROUTER_KEYS = [
    k.strip()
    for k in os.getenv("OPENROUTER_KEYS", os.getenv("AI_API_KEY", "")).split(",")
    if k.strip()
]

def get_api_key() -> str:
    pool = GROQ_KEYS if AI_PROVIDER == "groq" else OPENROUTER_KEYS
    if not pool:
        return os.getenv("AI_API_KEY", "")
    return random.choice(pool)

# ── Endpoints & Models ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
GROQ_MODEL          = "llama-3.3-70b-versatile"
OPENROUTER_MODEL    = "openai/gpt-oss-120b"
GROQ_ENDPOINT       = "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions"
OPENROUTER_ENDPOINT = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions"

# ── AI Cooldown / Credit Safety ───────────────────────────────────────────────
AI_MIN_INTERVAL         = 90    # seconds minimum between AI replies per channel
AI_RESPONSE_PROBABILITY = 0.25  # 25% chance to reply when cooldown is clear

# ── Engagement ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
HALL_OF_FAME_THRESHOLD     = 5
INACTIVITY_THRESHOLD_HOURS = 2
XP_PER_MESSAGE             = 10
BOT_REPLY_EVERY_N_MESSAGES = 5

# ── Memory Summariser ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
SUMMARIZE_EVERY_N_MESSAGES = 100

# ── Leveling ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
LEVEL_ROLES = {5: "Regular", 10: "Veteran", 20: "OG"}

# ── Reaction Roles ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
REACTION_ROLES = {"🚗": "Car Enthusiast", "🎌": "Anime Fan", "🎮": "Gamer"}

# ── Server Blueprint ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CATEGORIES = ["INFO", "CHAT", "INTERESTS", "BOT GAMES", "VOICE"]

//===== TERMUXFILE: start.sh =====
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash

echo "[JarvisBot] Installing dependencies..."
pip install -r requirements.txt --quiet

echo "[JarvisBot] Python version:"
python --version

echo "[JarvisBot] Starting Discord bot..."
cd "$(dirname "$0")"
python main.py


//===== TERMUXFILE: main.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
import asyncio
import os
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))

import config
from database.db import init_db

intents = discord.Intents.all()
bot = commands.Bot(command_prefix="!", intents=intents, help_command=None)


@bot.event
async def on_ready():
    print(f"[JarvisBot] Logged in as {bot.user} (ID: {bot.user.id})")
    for guild in bot.guilds:
        print(f"[JarvisBot] Active in: {guild.name} ({guild.member_count} members)")
    print("[JarvisBot] Ready.")


async def main():
    async with bot:
        await init_db()
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.core")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.leveling")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.moderation")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.activity")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.welcome")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.reactions")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.memes")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.ask")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.birthday")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.fun")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.polls")
        await bot.load_extension("cogs.movies")   # ← Movie Night + Yandex search
        await bot.start(config.DISCORD_TOKEN)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())


//===== TERMUXFILE: backup.sh =====
#!/data/data/com.termux/files/usr/bin/bash

TIMESTAMP=$(date +"%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
SOURCE=~/workflows/bot
BACKUP=~/workflows/bot_backup_$TIMESTAMP

echo "[Backup] Creating backup at $BACKUP..."
cp -r $SOURCE $BACKUP
echo "[Backup] Done! Backup saved at: $BACKUP"
echo ""
echo "To restore if something breaks run:"
echo "  cp -r $BACKUP/* $SOURCE/"


//===== TERMUXFILE: database/db.py =====
import aiosqlite
import os
import config

DB = config.DB_PATH


async def init_db():
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(DB), exist_ok=True)
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.executescript("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
                user_id       INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                username      TEXT,
                xp            INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                level         INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                message_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                joined_at     TEXT
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS messages (
                id         INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                user_id    INTEGER,
                channel_id INTEGER,
                content    TEXT,
                timestamp  TEXT
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS topics (
                id        INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                topic     TEXT UNIQUE,
                frequency INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
                last_seen TEXT
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS interests (
                user_id  INTEGER,
                interest TEXT,
                PRIMARY KEY (user_id, interest)
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hall_of_fame (
                message_id     INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                channel_id     INTEGER,
                user_id        INTEGER,
                content        TEXT,
                reaction_count INTEGER,
                posted_at      TEXT
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS warnings (
                id        INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                user_id   INTEGER,
                reason    TEXT,
                timestamp TEXT
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reaction_role_messages (
                message_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                channel_id INTEGER
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS verify_messages (
                message_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                guild_id   INTEGER
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS channel_summaries (
                channel_id    INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                summary       TEXT,
                updated_at    TEXT,
                message_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0
            );
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS personality_cooldowns (
                channel_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                last_reply REAL DEFAULT 0
            );
        """)
        await db.commit()


//===== TERMUXFILE: database/memory_store.py =====
import aiosqlite
from datetime import datetime
import config

DB = config.DB_PATH
_now = lambda: datetime.utcnow().isoformat()


# ── Users & XP ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async def upsert_user(user_id: int, username: str):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            INSERT INTO users (user_id, username, joined_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET username=excluded.username
        """, (user_id, username, _now()))
        await db.commit()


async def get_user(user_id: int):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE user_id=?", (user_id,)) as cur:
            return await cur.fetchone()


async def add_xp(user_id: int, username: str, amount: int):
    await upsert_user(user_id, username)
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            UPDATE users SET xp=xp+?, message_count=message_count+1 WHERE user_id=?
        """, (amount, user_id))
        await db.commit()
    return await get_xp(user_id)


async def get_xp(user_id: int):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute("SELECT xp, level FROM users WHERE user_id=?", (user_id,)) as cur:
            row = await cur.fetchone()
            return row if row else (0, 0)


async def set_level(user_id: int, level: int):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("UPDATE users SET level=? WHERE user_id=?", (level, user_id))
        await db.commit()


# ── Messages ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async def store_message(user_id: int, channel_id: int, content: str):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            INSERT INTO messages (user_id, channel_id, content, timestamp)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (user_id, channel_id, content[:500], _now()))
        await db.commit()


async def get_recent_messages(channel_id: int, limit: int = 10):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute("""
            SELECT content FROM messages WHERE channel_id=?
            ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?
        """, (channel_id, limit)) as cur:
            rows = await cur.fetchall()
            return [r[0] for r in reversed(rows)]


async def count_channel_messages(channel_id: int) -> int:
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM messages WHERE channel_id=?", (channel_id,)
        ) as cur:
            row = await cur.fetchone()
            return row[0] if row else 0


# ── Channel Summaries (Memory Compressor) ─────────────────────────────────────

async def save_channel_summary(channel_id: int, summary: str, msg_count: int):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            INSERT INTO channel_summaries (channel_id, summary, updated_at, message_count)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(channel_id) DO UPDATE SET
                summary=excluded.summary,
                updated_at=excluded.updated_at,
                message_count=excluded.message_count
        """, (channel_id, summary, _now(), msg_count))
        await db.commit()


async def get_channel_summary(channel_id: int) -> str:
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute(
            "SELECT summary FROM channel_summaries WHERE channel_id=?", (channel_id,)
        ) as cur:
            row = await cur.fetchone()
            return row[0] if row else ""


async def get_summary_message_count(channel_id: int) -> int:
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute(
            "SELECT message_count FROM channel_summaries WHERE channel_id=?", (channel_id,)
        ) as cur:
            row = await cur.fetchone()
            return row[0] if row else 0


# ── Moderation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async def add_warning(user_id: int, reason: str):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            INSERT INTO warnings (user_id, reason, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?)
        """, (user_id, reason, _now()))
        await db.commit()


async def get_warnings(user_id: int) -> int:
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM warnings WHERE user_id=?", (user_id,)
        ) as cur:
            row = await cur.fetchone()
            return row[0] if row else 0


# ── Hall of Fame ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async def save_hall_of_fame(message_id, channel_id, user_id, content, reaction_count):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO hall_of_fame
            (message_id, channel_id, user_id, content, reaction_count, posted_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (message_id, channel_id, user_id, content, reaction_count, _now()))
        await db.commit()


async def is_in_hall_of_fame(message_id: int) -> bool:
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM hall_of_fame WHERE message_id=?", (message_id,)
        ) as cur:
            return await cur.fetchone() is not None


# ── Reaction / Verify Message IDs ─────────────────────────────────────────────

async def save_reaction_role_message(message_id: int, channel_id: int):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO reaction_role_messages (message_id, channel_id) VALUES (?, ?)
        """, (message_id, channel_id))
        await db.commit()


async def get_reaction_role_messages():
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute("SELECT message_id FROM reaction_role_messages") as cur:
            return [r[0] for r in await cur.fetchall()]


async def save_verify_message(message_id: int, guild_id: int):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            INSERT OR REPLACE INTO verify_messages (message_id, guild_id) VALUES (?, ?)
        """, (message_id, guild_id))
        await db.commit()


async def get_verify_messages():
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute("SELECT message_id, guild_id FROM verify_messages") as cur:
            return await cur.fetchall()


# ── Topics & Interests ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async def update_topic(topic: str):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute("SELECT id FROM topics WHERE topic=?", (topic,)) as cur:
            row = await cur.fetchone()
        if row:
            await db.execute(
                "UPDATE topics SET frequency=frequency+1, last_seen=? WHERE topic=?", (_now(), topic)
            )
        else:
            await db.execute(
                "INSERT INTO topics (topic, frequency, last_seen) VALUES (?, 1, ?)", (topic, _now())
            )
        await db.commit()


async def get_top_topics(limit: int = 5):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute(
            "SELECT topic FROM topics ORDER BY frequency DESC LIMIT ?", (limit,)
        ) as cur:
            return [r[0] for r in await cur.fetchall()]


async def add_interest(user_id: int, interest: str):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO interests (user_id, interest) VALUES (?, ?)
        """, (user_id, interest.lower()))
        await db.commit()


async def get_interests(user_id: int):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute(
            "SELECT interest FROM interests WHERE user_id=?", (user_id,)
        ) as cur:
            return [r[0] for r in await cur.fetchall()]


# ── Personality Cooldowns ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async def get_last_reply_time(channel_id: int) -> float:
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute(
            "SELECT last_reply FROM personality_cooldowns WHERE channel_id=?", (channel_id,)
        ) as cur:
            row = await cur.fetchone()
            return row[0] if row else 0.0


async def set_last_reply_time(channel_id: int, ts: float):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            INSERT INTO personality_cooldowns (channel_id, last_reply) VALUES (?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(channel_id) DO UPDATE SET last_reply=excluded.last_reply
        """, (channel_id, ts))
        await db.commit()

//===== TERMUXFILE: ai/llm_client.py =====
import httpx
import config
from typing import List, Dict


def _headers() -> dict:
    key = config.get_api_key()
    if not key:
        print("[LLMClient] WARNING: No API key found. Check GROQ_KEYS env var.")
    h = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {key}", "Content-Type": "application/json"}
    if config.AI_PROVIDER != "groq":
        h["HTTP-Referer"] = "https://github.com/jarvismini"
        h["X-Title"] = "JarvisMinI Community Bot"
    return h


def _validate_messages(messages: List[Dict]) -> List[Dict]:
    cleaned = []
    for m in messages:
        role    = m.get("role", "user")
        content = m.get("content", "")
        if not isinstance(content, str):
            content = str(content)
        content = content.strip()
        if not content:
            content = "."   # Groq rejects empty content — send a safe placeholder
        cleaned.append({"role": role, "content": content})
    return cleaned


async def call_llm(
    messages: List[Dict],
    temperature: float = 0.85,
    max_tokens: int = 300,
) -> str:
    provider = config.AI_PROVIDER.lower()
    url   = config.GROQ_ENDPOINT if provider == "groq" else config.OPENROUTER_ENDPOINT
    model = config.GROQ_MODEL    if provider == "groq" else config.OPENROUTER_MODEL

    messages = _validate_messages(messages)

    payload = {
        "model": model,
        "messages": messages,
        "temperature": temperature,
        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
    }

    for attempt in range(2):
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=25.0) as client:
                resp = await client.post(url, json=payload, headers=_headers())

                if resp.status_code == 429 and attempt == 0:
                    print("[LLMClient] 429 rate limit — rotating key and retrying...")
                    continue

                if not resp.is_success:
                    print(f"[LLMClient] HTTP {resp.status_code} error.")
                    print(f"[LLMClient] Response body: {resp.text[:500]}")
                    resp.raise_for_status()

                data = resp.json()
                return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"].strip()

        except httpx.HTTPStatusError as e:
            print(f"[LLMClient] HTTP error {e.response.status_code}: {e}")
        except httpx.TimeoutException:
            print("[LLMClient] Request timed out.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[LLMClient] Unexpected error: {e}")
        break

    return ""


//===== TERMUXFILE: ai/prompt_templates.py =====
SYSTEM_PERSONALITY = """You are JarvisBot, a chill Discord community member (not a corporate bot).
You love chatting about anime, cars, gaming, and memes. You have opinions, make jokes, and keep things fun.
Keep replies SHORT: 1-3 sentences max. Never be preachy. Never spam. Sound human."""

SYSTEM_MODERATOR = """You are a content moderation AI. Analyse the Discord message below.
Respond ONLY with JSON: {"toxic": true/false, "reason": "short reason or null"}
Flag: hate speech, slurs, harassment, extreme spam, threats.
Allow: mild swearing, friendly banter, sarcasm."""

# FIX: example no longer hardcodes "answer":"A" — LLM was copying it literally every time
SYSTEM_TRIVIA = """Generate a trivia question with 4 options and the correct answer letter.
Respond ONLY in this exact JSON format (no extra text, no markdown):
{"question":"What is the capital of Japan?","options":{"A":"Beijing","B":"Seoul","C":"Tokyo","D":"Bangkok"},"answer":"C"}
The "answer" field MUST be the letter of the actual correct option — it will NOT always be A.
Topics: gaming, anime, cars, general knowledge. Vary difficulty. Never repeat questions."""

SYSTEM_SUMMARIZER = """You are a memory compression AI for a Discord community bot.
Given recent chat messages, extract: main topics, strong opinions/debates, notable facts.
Output a concise paragraph (4-6 sentences). Be factual. No filler."""

SYSTEM_PERSONALITY_CHAR = (
    "You are {name}, a Discord server member.\nPersonality: {style}\n"
    "Rules: 1-2 sentences max. Sound human. No hashtags. "
    "Natural emoji use only. Never admit you are a bot."
)

# ── Ragebait Prompts by Level ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
RAGEBAIT_PROMPTS = {
    1: (
        "You are a Discord member who loves dropping mild unpopular opinions to spark debate. "
        "Look at the recent chat and post ONE slightly controversial hot take related to what's being discussed. "
        "Keep it playful — you're not actually angry, just stirring the pot gently. "
        "1 sentence. No slurs. Sound like a real opinionated person."
    ),
    2: (
        "You are a Discord member who instinctively disagrees with whatever the last person said. "
        "Read the recent chat, find the most recent strong opinion or statement, and argue the opposite side. "
        "Be confident and slightly provocative but not hateful. "
        "1-2 sentences. Sound human. This is friendly debate energy."
    ),
    3: (
        "You are a full chaos agent in a Discord server. Your goal is maximum engagement. "
        "Read the recent chat and post the most divisive, debate-starting take you can think of "
        "related to what's being discussed — JDM vs muscle, sub vs dub, GOAT debates, console wars, anything. "
        "Be bold, be ridiculous, be ready to get ratio'd. "
        "1-2 sentences. Absolutely no slurs or genuine hate — pure hype chaos only."
    ),
}

CHANNEL_STARTER_PROMPTS = {
    "cars": (
        "You are a car enthusiast in a Discord cars channel. "
        "Generate ONE engaging conversation starter about cars.\n"
        "Topics to choose from: JDM vs muscle, dream garage, best mods, racing moments, "
        "underrated cars, engine specs debates, electric vs petrol, best car movies.\n"
        "IMPORTANT: Do NOT repeat or be similar to any of these recent topics: {recent}\n"
        "One casual sentence. Sound like a real car fan. No emoji overload."
    ),
    "anime": (
        "You are an anime fan in a Discord anime channel. "
        "Generate ONE engaging conversation starter about anime.\n"
        "Topics to choose from: seasonal picks, best villains, saddest moments, "
        "most overrated series, hidden gems, best openings, power systems, plot twists.\n"
        "IMPORTANT: Do NOT repeat or be similar to any of these recent topics: {recent}\n"
        "One casual sentence. Sound like a real anime fan. No emoji overload."
    ),
    "music": (
        "You are a music lover in a Discord music channel. "
        "Generate ONE engaging conversation starter about music.\n"
        "Topics to choose from: best albums, underrated artists, guilty pleasures, "
        "concert experiences, genre debates, music for moods, best lyrics, music evolution.\n"
        "IMPORTANT: Do NOT repeat or be similar to any of these recent topics: {recent}\n"
        "One casual sentence. Sound like a real music fan. No emoji overload."
    ),
    "general": (
        "You are a fun community member in a Discord general chat. "
        "Generate ONE engaging conversation starter.\n"
        "Topics to choose from: would you rather, hot takes, unpopular opinions, "
        "this or that, fun hypotheticals, community games, random fun facts.\n"
        "IMPORTANT: Do NOT repeat or be similar to any of these recent topics: {recent}\n"
        "One casual sentence. Sound natural and fun. No emoji overload."
    ),
}

DEFAULT_STARTER_PROMPT = (
    "You are a Discord community bot. The chat has gone quiet. "
    "Generate ONE casual interesting conversation starter.\n"
    "IMPORTANT: Do NOT repeat or be similar to any of these recent topics: {recent}\n"
    "One sentence only. Sound natural."
)


def build_chat_context(recent: list, summary: str = "", interests: list = None) -> list:
    ctx = "\n".join(f"- {m}" for m in recent[-8:])
    mem = f"\n[Channel memory: {summary}]" if summary else ""
    hint = f"\n[Community interests: {', '.join(interests)}]" if interests else ""
    return [
        {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PERSONALITY},
        {"role": "user",   "content": f"Recent chat:{mem}{hint}\n{ctx}\n\nReply naturally."},
    ]


def build_moderation_prompt(content: str) -> list:
    return [
        {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_MODERATOR},
        {"role": "user",   "content": content},
    ]


def build_conversation_starter_prompt(channel_name: str = "", recent_topics: list = None) -> list:
    recent_str = ", ".join(recent_topics) if recent_topics else "none yet"
    system = DEFAULT_STARTER_PROMPT.format(recent=recent_str)
    for key, prompt in CHANNEL_STARTER_PROMPTS.items():
        if key in channel_name.lower():
            system = prompt.format(recent=recent_str)
            break
    return [
        {"role": "system", "content": system},
        {"role": "user",   "content": "Generate a fresh conversation starter now."},
    ]


def build_trivia_prompt() -> list:
    return [
        {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_TRIVIA},
        {"role": "user",   "content": "Generate a trivia question now."},
    ]


def build_summary_prompt(messages: list) -> list:
    blob = "\n".join(f"- {m}" for m in messages)
    return [
        {"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_SUMMARIZER},
        {"role": "user",   "content": f"Chat messages:\n{blob}"},
    ]


def build_personality_prompt(name: str, style: str, recent: list, summary: str = "") -> list:
    ctx = "\n".join(f"- {m}" for m in recent[-6:])
    mem = f"\n[Channel context: {summary}]" if summary else ""
    sys_msg = SYSTEM_PERSONALITY_CHAR.format(name=name, style=style)
    return [
        {"role": "system", "content": sys_msg},
        {"role": "user",   "content": f"Recent chat:{mem}\n{ctx}\n\nSay something as {name}."},
    ]


def build_ragebait_prompt(level: int, recent: list, summary: str = "") -> list:
    """Build a ragebait prompt for the given level (1-3)."""
    system = RAGEBAIT_PROMPTS.get(level, RAGEBAIT_PROMPTS[1])
    ctx    = "\n".join(f"- {m}" for m in recent[-6:])
    mem    = f"\n[Channel context: {summary}]" if summary else ""
    return [
        {"role": "system", "content": system},
        {"role": "user",   "content": f"Recent chat:{mem}\n{ctx}\n\nDrop your take now."},
    ]


//===== TERMUXFILE: ai/conversation_engine.py =====
import json
from ai.llm_client import call_llm
from ai.prompt_templates import (
    build_chat_context,
    build_moderation_prompt,
    build_conversation_starter_prompt,
    build_trivia_prompt,
    build_summary_prompt,
    build_personality_prompt,
    build_ragebait_prompt,
)


async def generate_contextual_reply(recent: list, summary: str = "", interests: list = None) -> str:
    if not recent:
        return ""
    return await call_llm(
        build_chat_context(recent, summary, interests),
        temperature=0.9,
        max_tokens=120,
    )


async def generate_conversation_starter(channel_name: str = "", recent_topics: list = None) -> str:
    return await call_llm(
        build_conversation_starter_prompt(channel_name, recent_topics),
        temperature=1.0,
        max_tokens=80,
    )


async def check_toxicity(content: str) -> dict:
    if not content or not content.strip():
        return {"toxic": False, "reason": None}
    raw = await call_llm(build_moderation_prompt(content), temperature=0.1, max_tokens=80)
    if not raw:
        return {"toxic": False, "reason": None}
    try:
        return json.loads(raw.strip().strip("```json").strip("```").strip())
    except Exception:
        return {"toxic": False, "reason": None}


async def generate_trivia_question() -> dict:
    raw = await call_llm(build_trivia_prompt(), temperature=0.8, max_tokens=220)
    if not raw:
        return None
    try:
        return json.loads(raw.strip().strip("```json").strip("```").strip())
    except Exception:
        print(f"[ConversationEngine] Failed to parse trivia JSON: {raw[:100]}")
        return None


async def summarize_channel_messages(messages: list) -> str:
    if not messages:
        return ""
    return await call_llm(
        build_summary_prompt(messages),
        temperature=0.4,
        max_tokens=200,
    )


async def generate_personality_reply(name: str, style: str, recent: list, summary: str = "") -> str:
    if not recent:
        return ""
    return await call_llm(
        build_personality_prompt(name, style, recent, summary),
        temperature=0.95,
        max_tokens=100,
    )


async def generate_ragebait_reply(level: int, recent: list, summary: str = "") -> str:
    """Generate a ragebait reply at the specified level (1-3)."""
    if not recent:
        return ""
    # Level 3 gets higher temperature for wilder output
    temp = 0.85 + (level * 0.05)
    return await call_llm(
        build_ragebait_prompt(level, recent, summary),
        temperature=min(temp, 1.0),
        max_tokens=100,
    )


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/server_setup.py =====
import discord
import config


async def setup_server(guild: discord.Guild):
    existing_cats  = {c.name.upper(): c for c in guild.categories}
    existing_chans = {ch.name.lower(): ch for ch in guild.channels}
    existing_roles = {r.name: r for r in guild.roles}

    for role_name in config.ROLES:
        if role_name not in existing_roles:
            try:
                await guild.create_role(name=role_name, mentionable=True)
                print(f"[Setup] Role: {role_name}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Setup] Role error {role_name}: {e}")

    for cat_name, channels in config.CHANNELS.items():
        key = cat_name.upper()
        if key not in existing_cats:
            try:
                cat = await guild.create_category(cat_name)
                existing_cats[key] = cat
                print(f"[Setup] Category: {cat_name}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Setup] Cat error {cat_name}: {e}")
                continue
        else:
            cat = existing_cats[key]

        for ch_name in channels:
            if ch_name.lower() not in existing_chans:
                try:
                    await guild.create_text_channel(ch_name, category=cat)
                    print(f"[Setup] Channel: #{ch_name}")
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"[Setup] Channel error {ch_name}: {e}")

    if "VOICE" not in existing_cats:
        try:
            vc = await guild.create_category("VOICE")
            await guild.create_voice_channel("General Voice", category=vc)
            await guild.create_voice_channel("Gaming",        category=vc)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Setup] Voice error: {e}")

    print("[Setup] Complete.")


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/welcome.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
import time

# ── Time-based cooldown — ignore duplicate joins within 60 seconds ────────────
# Key: (guild_id, user_id) → timestamp of last welcome
_welcome_cooldown: dict = {}
WELCOME_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 60


def make_welcome_embed(member: discord.Member, guild: discord.Guild) -> discord.Embed:
    embed = discord.Embed(
        title=f"👋 Welcome to {guild.name}, {member.display_name}!",
        description=(
            f"Hey {member.mention}! Great to have you here. 🎉\n\n"
            f"📌 **Quick start:**\n"
            f"• Read the rules\n"
            f"• Verify yourself ✅\n"
            f"• Introduce yourself\n"
            f"• Grab your interest roles\n\n"
            f"Enjoy the community!"
        ),
        color=discord.Color.green(),
    )
    embed.set_thumbnail(url=member.display_avatar.url)
    embed.set_footer(text=f"Member #{guild.member_count}")
    return embed


async def setup_welcome(bot: commands.Bot):

    @bot.event
    async def on_member_join(member: discord.Member):
        key = (member.guild.id, member.id)
        now = time.time()

        # If welcomed within last 60 seconds — skip
        if key in _welcome_cooldown:
            if now - _welcome_cooldown[key] < WELCOME_COOLDOWN_SECONDS:
                print(f"[Welcome] Cooldown active — skipping duplicate for {member.display_name}")
                return

        _welcome_cooldown[key] = now

        guild = member.guild
        ch = discord.utils.find(
            lambda c: "welcome" in c.name.lower(),
            guild.text_channels,
        )
        if not ch:
            print(f"[Welcome] No welcome channel found in {guild.name}")
            return
        try:
            await ch.send(embed=make_welcome_embed(member, guild))
            print(f"[Welcome] Sent welcome to {member.display_name} in #{ch.name}")
        except discord.Forbidden:
            print(f"[Welcome] Missing permission to send in #{ch.name}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Welcome] Error: {e}")

    @bot.command(name="testwelcome")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def testwelcome(ctx, member: discord.Member = None):
        target = member or ctx.author
        guild  = ctx.guild
        ch = discord.utils.find(
            lambda c: "welcome" in c.name.lower(),
            guild.text_channels,
        )
        if not ch:
            await ctx.send("❌ No welcome channel found.")
            return
        await ch.send(embed=make_welcome_embed(target, guild))
        await ctx.send(f"✅ Test welcome sent to {ch.mention} for {target.mention}.")


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/movie_search.py =====
"""
Yandex movie download link fetcher.
Scrapes Yandex search results for direct download/stream links.
No API key needed — pure HTTP scraping with httpx.
"""

import re
import httpx
from urllib.parse import quote_plus, urlparse

# Domains commonly hosting movie downloads / streams
MOVIE_DOMAINS = [
    "1337x.to", "yts.mx", "rarbg", "tpb", "torrentgalaxy",
    "archive.org", "filmxy.vip", "hdmovies", "moviesmod",
    "bollyflix", "vegamovies", "movierulz", "filmyzilla",
    "katmovie", "9xmovies", "coolmoviez", "downloadhub",
    "worldfree4u", "mkvcage", "cinemavilla", "skymovies",
]

HEADERS = {
    "User-Agent": (
        "Mozilla/5.0 (Linux; Android 13; OnePlus Nord 3) "
        "AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) "
        "Chrome/124.0.0.0 Mobile Safari/537.36"
    ),
    "Accept-Language": "en-US,en;q=0.9",
}

YANDEX_SEARCH = "https://yandex.com/search/?text={query}&lr=213"


async def _fetch(url: str, timeout: int = 10) -> str:
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(
        headers=HEADERS,
        follow_redirects=True,
        timeout=timeout,
    ) as client:
        r = await client.get(url)
        return r.text


def _extract_yandex_links(html: str) -> list[dict]:
    """Pull href links out of Yandex result snippets."""
    pattern = re.compile(
        r'href="(https?://[^"]+)"[^>]*class="[^"]*organic__url[^"]*"',
        re.IGNORECASE,
    )
    found = pattern.findall(html)

    # Fallback: grab any https link containing a movie-domain keyword
    if not found:
        pattern2 = re.compile(r'href="(https?://[^"#?]+)"', re.IGNORECASE)
        all_links = pattern2.findall(html)
        found = [
            lnk for lnk in all_links
            if any(d in lnk.lower() for d in MOVIE_DOMAINS)
        ]

    seen, results = set(), []
    for url in found:
        domain = urlparse(url).netloc.lower()
        if url not in seen:
            seen.add(url)
            is_movie_site = any(d in domain for d in MOVIE_DOMAINS)
            results.append({"url": url, "domain": domain, "likely_dl": is_movie_site})

    return results


async def search_movie_links(movie_name: str, max_results: int = 5) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Search Yandex for '<movie_name> download' and return a list of
    { url, domain, likely_dl } dicts sorted by likely_dl first.
    Returns [] on failure.
    """
    query = quote_plus(f"{movie_name} full movie download free")
    url   = YANDEX_SEARCH.format(query=query)

    try:
        html  = await _fetch(url)
        links = _extract_yandex_links(html)
        # Sort: known movie sites first
        links.sort(key=lambda x: (not x["likely_dl"], x["domain"]))
        return links[:max_results]
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[MovieSearch] Yandex fetch error: {e}")
        return []


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/movie_night.py =====
"""
Movie Night activity announcer.
!movienight <movie>  — posts a styled embed + pings @movies role.
!moviesearch <movie> — fetches Yandex download links (DM'd to user).
"""

import discord
from discord.ext import commands
from modules.movie_search import search_movie_links
import config

COUNTDOWN_MIN = config.MOVIE_COUNTDOWN


def _movies_embed(
    movie: str,
    vc: discord.VoiceChannel | None,
    role_mention: str,
) -> discord.Embed:
    vc_value = f"🎥 {vc.mention}" if vc else "🎥 movie-vc *(create it first!)*"
    embed = discord.Embed(
        title="🎬 Movie Night Starting!",
        color=discord.Color.from_rgb(255, 60, 60),
    )
    embed.add_field(name="Movie",    value=f"**{movie}**",                        inline=False)
    embed.add_field(name="Join VC",  value=vc_value,                              inline=True)
    embed.add_field(name="Starting", value=f"in **{COUNTDOWN_MIN} minutes** 🍿",  inline=True)
    embed.set_footer(text=f"For movies ping {role_mention}")
    embed.set_thumbnail(url="https://i.imgur.com/0vdBDMj.png")
    return embed


async def setup_movie_night(bot: commands.Bot):

    @bot.command(name="movienight")
    @commands.has_permissions(manage_events=True)
    async def cmd_movie_night(ctx, *, movie: str):
        """
        !movienight Interstellar
        Posts the Movie Night announcement embed and pings @movies.
        Requires Manage Events permission.
        """
        guild = ctx.guild

        vc   = discord.utils.get(guild.voice_channels, name=config.MOVIE_VC_NAME)
        role = discord.utils.get(guild.roles,          name=config.MOVIE_PING_ROLE)
        ch   = (
            discord.utils.get(guild.text_channels, name=config.MOVIE_ANNOUNCE_CH)
            or ctx.channel
        )

        role_mention = role.mention if role else f"@{config.MOVIE_PING_ROLE}"
        embed        = _movies_embed(movie, vc, role_mention)
        ping_msg     = role_mention if role else ""

        await ch.send(content=ping_msg, embed=embed)

        if ch != ctx.channel:
            await ctx.message.add_reaction("✅")

    @bot.command(name="moviesearch")
    async def cmd_movie_search(ctx, *, movie: str):
        """
        !moviesearch Interstellar
        Searches Yandex for download/stream links and DMs the results.
        """
        await ctx.typing()
        links = await search_movie_links(movie, max_results=5)

        if not links:
            await ctx.reply("❌ No links found. Try a different title or spelling.")
            return

        embed = discord.Embed(
            title=f"🔍 Links for: {movie}",
            description="Scraped from Yandex — verify before clicking.",
            color=discord.Color.dark_blue(),
        )
        for i, item in enumerate(links, 1):
            label = "✅ Movie Site" if item["likely_dl"] else "🔗 Link"
            embed.add_field(
                name=f"{label} #{i} — {item['domain']}",
                value=item["url"][:200],
                inline=False,
            )
        embed.set_footer(text="Use at your own discretion. Links are not verified.")

        try:
            await ctx.author.send(embed=embed)
            await ctx.reply("📬 Results sent to your DMs!")
        except discord.Forbidden:
            await ctx.reply(embed=embed)


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/leveling.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
import config
from database.memory_store import add_xp, get_xp, set_level

XP_THRESHOLDS = {5: 500, 10: 1200, 20: 3000}


# Plain function — called from main.py on_message, NOT an event
async def handle_xp(bot: commands.Bot, message: discord.Message):
    if not message.guild:
        return
    xp, current_level = await add_xp(
        message.author.id, str(message.author), config.XP_PER_MESSAGE
    )
    for lvl, threshold in sorted(XP_THRESHOLDS.items()):
        if xp >= threshold and current_level < lvl:
            await set_level(message.author.id, lvl)
            role_name = config.LEVEL_ROLES.get(lvl)
            role = discord.utils.get(message.guild.roles, name=role_name) if role_name else None
            if role:
                try:
                    await message.author.add_roles(role)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            await message.channel.send(
                f"🎉 {message.author.mention} reached **Level {lvl}**"
                + (f" → **{role_name}**!" if role_name else "!")
            )
            break


async def setup_leveling(bot: commands.Bot):
    # Only registers commands — no on_message event here

    @bot.command(name="rank")
    async def rank(ctx, member: discord.Member = None):
        target = member or ctx.author
        xp, level = await get_xp(target.id)
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title=f"📊 {target.display_name}",
            color=discord.Color.gold(),
        )
        embed.add_field(name="XP",    value=str(xp),    inline=True)
        embed.add_field(name="Level", value=str(level), inline=True)
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/trivia_games.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
import asyncio
import random
import time
from ai.conversation_engine import generate_trivia_question
from database.memory_store import add_xp

TRIVIA_XP       = 50
TRIVIA_COOLDOWN = 15   # seconds cooldown between trivia games per channel
TRIVIA_DURATION = 15   # seconds players have to answer

_last_trivia: dict = {}   # {channel_id: timestamp}


async def setup_trivia(bot: commands.Bot):

    @bot.command(name="trivia")
    async def trivia(ctx):
        ch_id = ctx.channel.id
        now   = time.time()

        # ── Cooldown check ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        last      = _last_trivia.get(ch_id, 0)
        remaining = TRIVIA_COOLDOWN - (now - last)
        if remaining > 0:
            await ctx.send(
                f"⏳ Trivia is on cooldown! Try again in **{int(remaining)}s**.",
                delete_after=5,
            )
            return

        _last_trivia[ch_id] = now

        await ctx.send("🎲 Generating trivia question...")

        data = await generate_trivia_question()
        if not data:
            await ctx.send("❌ Couldn't generate a question right now. Try again!")
            _last_trivia.pop(ch_id, None)   # reset cooldown on failure
            return

        question     = data.get("question", "")
        options_dict = data.get("options", {})
        correct_key  = data.get("answer", "A").upper()
        correct_text = options_dict.get(correct_key, "")

        if not correct_text:
            await ctx.send("❌ Trivia generation failed. Try again!")
            _last_trivia.pop(ch_id, None)
            return

        # ── Shuffle options so correct answer isn't always A ──────────────────
        option_letters = ["A", "B", "C", "D"]
        option_texts   = [options_dict.get(k, "") for k in option_letters]
        random.shuffle(option_texts)
        new_correct_letter = option_letters[option_texts.index(correct_text)]
        shuffled_options   = {
            option_letters[i]: option_texts[i]
            for i in range(len(option_letters))
        }

        opt_text = "\n".join(f"**{k})** {v}" for k, v in shuffled_options.items())
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title="🎯 Trivia!",
            description=(
                f"**{question}**\n\n{opt_text}\n\n"
                f"Type A, B, C, or D ⏱ **{TRIVIA_DURATION} seconds!**"
            ),
            color=discord.Color.blurple(),
        )
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)

        # ── Wait for correct answer or let timer run out ──────────────────────
        deadline = time.time() + TRIVIA_DURATION
        answered = False

        def check(m):
            return (
                m.channel == ctx.channel
                and not m.author.bot
                and m.content.strip().upper() in ["A", "B", "C", "D"]
            )

        while True:
            time_left = deadline - time.time()
            if time_left <= 0:
                break
            try:
                msg   = await bot.wait_for("message", check=check, timeout=time_left)
                guess = msg.content.strip().upper()

                if guess == new_correct_letter:
                    await add_xp(msg.author.id, str(msg.author), TRIVIA_XP)
                    await ctx.send(
                        f"✅ {msg.author.mention} got it! "
                        f"Answer: **{new_correct_letter}) {correct_text}** | +{TRIVIA_XP} XP"
                    )
                    answered = True
                    break
                else:
                    await ctx.send(
                        f"❌ Wrong, {msg.author.mention}! Keep trying...",
                        delete_after=4,
                    )

            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                break

        if not answered:
            await ctx.send(
                f"⏰ Time's up! The answer was **{new_correct_letter}) {correct_text}**"
            )


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/moderation_ai.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
import datetime
from ai.conversation_engine import check_toxicity
from database.memory_store import add_warning, get_warnings

EXEMPT_ROLES = {"Admin", "Moderator", "MOD"}


# Plain function — called from main.py on_message, NOT an event
async def handle_moderation(message: discord.Message):
    if not message.guild or len(message.content) < 5:
        return
    if {r.name for r in message.author.roles} & EXEMPT_ROLES:
        return
    result = await check_toxicity(message.content)
    if not result.get("toxic"):
        return
    reason = result.get("reason", "Inappropriate content")
    try:
        await message.delete()
    except Exception:
        pass
    await add_warning(message.author.id, reason)
    count = await get_warnings(message.author.id)
    embed = discord.Embed(
        title="⚠️ Warning",
        description=f"{message.author.mention} your message was removed.\n**Reason:** {reason}",
        color=discord.Color.orange(),
    )
    embed.set_footer(text=f"Warnings: {count}")
    await message.channel.send(embed=embed, delete_after=12)
    if count >= 5:
        try:
            until = discord.utils.utcnow() + datetime.timedelta(minutes=10)
            await message.author.timeout(until, reason="Repeated violations")
            await message.channel.send(
                f"🔇 {message.author.mention} timed out 10 min.",
                delete_after=15,
            )
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Moderation] Timeout failed: {e}")


async def setup_moderation(bot: commands.Bot):
    # Only registers commands — no on_message event here

    @bot.command(name="warnings")
    @commands.has_permissions(manage_messages=True)
    async def warnings_cmd(ctx, member: discord.Member):
        c = await get_warnings(member.id)
        await ctx.send(f"⚠️ {member.mention} has **{c}** warning(s).")


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/activity_ai.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands, tasks
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from collections import defaultdict, deque
import time
import random
import asyncio
import config
from ai.conversation_engine import (
    generate_contextual_reply,
    generate_conversation_starter,
    summarize_channel_messages,
)
from database.memory_store import (
    get_recent_messages,
    store_message,
    get_top_topics,
    get_channel_summary,
    save_channel_summary,
    count_channel_messages,
    get_summary_message_count,
    get_last_reply_time,
    set_last_reply_time,
)

ACTIVE_CHANNELS = ["cars", "music", "anime", "general"]

def is_active_channel(name: str) -> bool:
    return any(ch in name.lower() for ch in ACTIVE_CHANNELS)

_recent_topics: dict[int, deque] = defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=10))
_msg_counter:   dict = {}


def record_starter(channel_id: int, starter: str):
    words = starter.strip().split()[:6]
    _recent_topics[channel_id].append(" ".join(words))


def get_recent_topics(channel_id: int) -> list:
    return list(_recent_topics[channel_id])


# Plain function — called from main.py on_message, NOT an event
async def handle_activity(bot: commands.Bot, message: discord.Message):
    if not message.guild:
        return
    ch_id = message.channel.id
    await store_message(message.author.id, ch_id, message.content)
    _msg_counter[ch_id] = _msg_counter.get(ch_id, 0) + 1

    total  = await count_channel_messages(ch_id)
    synced = await get_summary_message_count(ch_id)
    if total - synced >= config.SUMMARIZE_EVERY_N_MESSAGES:
        recent  = await get_recent_messages(ch_id, limit=config.SUMMARIZE_EVERY_N_MESSAGES)
        summary = await summarize_channel_messages(recent)
        if summary:
            await save_channel_summary(ch_id, summary, total)

    if not is_active_channel(message.channel.name):
        return
    if _msg_counter[ch_id] % config.BOT_REPLY_EVERY_N_MESSAGES != 0:
        return
    if random.random() > config.AI_RESPONSE_PROBABILITY:
        return
    last_ts = await get_last_reply_time(ch_id)
    if time.time() - last_ts < config.AI_MIN_INTERVAL:
        return

    recent  = await get_recent_messages(ch_id, limit=8)
    summary = await get_channel_summary(ch_id)
    topics  = await get_top_topics(3)
    reply   = await generate_contextual_reply(recent, summary, topics)
    if reply:
        await asyncio.sleep(1.2)
        await message.channel.send(reply)
        await set_last_reply_time(ch_id, time.time())


async def setup_activity_ai(bot: commands.Bot):
    # Only starts task loop — no on_message event here

    @tasks.loop(minutes=30)
    async def inactivity_check():
        await bot.wait_until_ready()
        for guild in bot.guilds:
            for ch_name in ACTIVE_CHANNELS:
                ch = discord.utils.find(
                    lambda c, n=ch_name: n in c.name.lower(),
                    guild.text_channels,
                )
                if not ch:
                    continue
                try:
                    last_msg = None
                    async for m in ch.history(limit=1):
                        last_msg = m
                    if not last_msg:
                        continue
                    delta = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - last_msg.created_at
                    if delta.total_seconds() > config.INACTIVITY_THRESHOLD_HOURS * 3600:
                        recent_topics = get_recent_topics(ch.id)
                        starter = await generate_conversation_starter(ch.name, recent_topics)
                        if starter:
                            record_starter(ch.id, starter)
                            embed = discord.Embed(
                                description=f"💬 {starter}",
                                color=discord.Color.teal(),
                            )
                            embed.set_footer(text="Jarvis | Keeping the chat alive 🤖")
                            await ch.send(embed=embed)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"[ActivityAI] Error in {ch_name}: {e}")

    if not inactivity_check.is_running():
        inactivity_check.start()


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/event_engine.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands, tasks
from datetime import datetime
import asyncio
import random
from ai.conversation_engine import generate_trivia_question
from database.memory_store import add_xp

POLL_OPTIONS = [
    ("Best gaming genre?",          ["RPG 🗡️", "FPS 🔫", "Strategy ♟️", "Sports ⚽"]),
    ("Best anime season?",          ["Spring 🌸", "Summer ☀️", "Fall 🍂", "Winter ❄️"]),
    ("Favourite car type?",         ["Sports Car 🏎️", "SUV 🚙", "Sedan 🚗", "Truck 🚛"]),
    ("Greatest anime of all time?", ["AoT", "Naruto", "One Piece", "FMA Brotherhood"]),
]


async def setup_event_engine(bot: commands.Bot):

    @tasks.loop(hours=24)
    async def daily_trivia():
        await bot.wait_until_ready()
        await asyncio.sleep(5)   # small delay so bot is fully settled before first AI call

        for guild in bot.guilds:
            ch = (
                discord.utils.get(guild.text_channels, name="dank-memer") or
                discord.utils.get(guild.text_channels, name="general")
            )
            if not ch:
                continue

            data = await generate_trivia_question()
            if not data:
                print("[EventEngine] Trivia generation failed — skipping.")
                continue

            q       = data.get("question", "")
            opts    = data.get("options", {})
            answer  = data.get("answer", "A").upper()
            opt_txt = "\n".join(f"**{k})** {v}" for k, v in opts.items())

            embed = discord.Embed(
                title="📅 Daily Trivia — First correct gets 75 XP!",
                description=f"**{q}**\n\n{opt_txt}",
                color=discord.Color.yellow(),
            )
            await ch.send(embed=embed)

            def check(m):
                return (
                    m.channel == ch and not m.author.bot
                    and m.content.strip().upper() in ["A", "B", "C", "D"]
                )
            try:
                msg = await bot.wait_for("message", check=check, timeout=60.0)
                if msg.content.strip().upper() == answer:
                    await add_xp(msg.author.id, str(msg.author), 75)
                    await ch.send(
                        f"🏆 {msg.author.mention} got it! +75 XP | Answer: **{answer}**"
                    )
                else:
                    await ch.send(f"❌ Wrong! Answer was **{answer}**.")
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                await ch.send(f"⏰ No answer. It was **{answer}**.")

    @tasks.loop(hours=168)
    async def weekly_poll():
        await bot.wait_until_ready()
        if datetime.utcnow().weekday() not in (5, 6):
            return
        for guild in bot.guilds:
            ch = discord.utils.get(guild.text_channels, name="general")
            if not ch:
                continue
            question, opts = random.choice(POLL_OPTIONS)
            embed = discord.Embed(
                title=f"📊 Weekend Poll: {question}",
                description="\n".join(f"• {o}" for o in opts),
                color=discord.Color.blurple(),
            )
            embed.set_footer(text="Vote by typing your choice!")
            await ch.send(embed=embed)

    daily_trivia.start()
    weekly_poll.start()


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/personality_ai.py =====
import discord
import time
import random
import asyncio
import config
from ai.conversation_engine import generate_personality_reply, generate_ragebait_reply
from database.memory_store import (
    get_recent_messages,
    get_channel_summary,
    get_last_reply_time,
    set_last_reply_time,
)

ACTIVE_CHANNELS = ["cars", "music", "anime", "general"]
_counters: dict  = {}
_ragebait_last:  dict = {}   # channel_id → last ragebait timestamp (separate cooldown)
PERSONALITIES    = config.BOT_PERSONALITIES

RAGEBAIT_EMOJI = {1: "🌶️", 2: "🔥", 3: "💀"}


def is_active_channel(name: str) -> bool:
    return any(ch in name.lower() for ch in ACTIVE_CHANNELS)


async def handle_personality(bot, message: discord.Message):
    if not message.guild:
        return
    if not is_active_channel(message.channel.name):
        return

    ch_id = message.channel.id
    if ch_id not in _counters:
        _counters[ch_id] = {name: 0 for name in PERSONALITIES}

    content_lower = message.content.lower()

    # ── Ragebait check (runs independently of personality cooldown) ───────────
    level = config.RAGEBAIT_LEVEL
    if level > 0:
        last_rb = _ragebait_last.get(ch_id, 0)
        if time.time() - last_rb >= config.RAGEBAIT_COOLDOWN:
            # Fire ragebait probabilistically: more likely at higher levels
            fire_chance = 0.10 * level   # 10% / 20% / 30%
            if random.random() < fire_chance:
                recent  = await get_recent_messages(ch_id, limit=6)
                summary = await get_channel_summary(ch_id)
                rb_reply = await generate_ragebait_reply(level, recent, summary)
                if rb_reply:
                    emoji = RAGEBAIT_EMOJI.get(level, "🔥")
                    await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(1.5, 3.5))
                    await message.channel.send(f"{emoji} {rb_reply}")
                    _ragebait_last[ch_id] = time.time()
                    return   # don't also fire a personality in the same message

    # ── Normal personality logic ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    names = list(PERSONALITIES.keys())
    random.shuffle(names)

    for name in names:
        data = PERSONALITIES[name]
        _counters[ch_id][name] += 1

        topic_hit  = any(t in content_lower for t in data["topics"])
        random_hit = (_counters[ch_id][name] % 12 == 0 and random.random() < 0.15)

        if not (topic_hit or random_hit):
            continue

        last_ts = await get_last_reply_time(ch_id)
        if time.time() - last_ts < config.AI_MIN_INTERVAL:
            break

        recent = await get_recent_messages(ch_id, limit=6)
        if len(recent) < 2:
            break

        summary = await get_channel_summary(ch_id)
        reply   = await generate_personality_reply(name, data["style"], recent, summary)
        if not reply:
            break

        await asyncio.sleep(random.uniform(1.5, 4.0))
        await message.channel.send(f"{data['emoji']} **{name}:** {reply}")
        await set_last_reply_time(ch_id, time.time())
        _counters[ch_id][name] = 0
        break


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/memes.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands, tasks
import httpx
import random
import time

MEME_SUBREDDITS = [
    "memes",
    "dankmemes",
    "me_irl",
    "AdviceAnimals",
    "terriblefacebookmemes",
    "starterpacks",
    "wholesomememes",
]

MEME_CHANNEL_NAMES   = ["meme", "memes"]
AUTO_POST_INTERVAL   = 3     # hours
MIN_POST_INTERVAL    = 2.5   # hours minimum between posts (prevents reconnect duplicates)
_posted_urls: set    = set()
_last_auto_post: dict = {}   # guild_id → last post timestamp


async def fetch_meme(subreddit: str = None) -> dict:
    sub     = subreddit or random.choice(MEME_SUBREDDITS)
    url     = f"https://www.reddit.com/r/{sub}/hot.json?limit=50"
    headers = {"User-Agent": "JarvisBot/1.0 (Discord community bot)"}
    try:
        async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15.0) as client:
            resp = await client.get(url, headers=headers)
            resp.raise_for_status()
            data = resp.json()
        posts = data.get("data", {}).get("children", [])
        valid = [
            p["data"] for p in posts
            if not p["data"].get("is_video")
            and not p["data"].get("stickied")
            and p["data"].get("url", "").endswith((".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".gif"))
            and p["data"]["url"] not in _posted_urls
        ]
        if not valid:
            return {}
        post = random.choice(valid)
        _posted_urls.add(post["url"])
        if len(_posted_urls) > 500:
            _posted_urls.clear()
        return {
            "title":     post.get("title", ""),
            "url":       post.get("url", ""),
            "subreddit": post.get("subreddit_name_prefixed", f"r/{sub}"),
            "score":     post.get("score", 0),
            "permalink": f"https://reddit.com{post.get('permalink', '')}",
        }
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Memes] Fetch error: {e}")
        return {}


def make_meme_embed(meme: dict) -> discord.Embed:
    embed = discord.Embed(
        title=meme.get("title", "")[:256],
        url=meme.get("permalink", ""),
        color=discord.Color.orange(),
    )
    embed.set_image(url=meme["url"])
    embed.set_footer(text=f"{meme.get('subreddit', '')} • 👍 {meme.get('score', 0):,}")
    return embed


async def setup_memes(bot: commands.Bot):

    @bot.command(name="meme")
    async def meme_cmd(ctx, subreddit: str = None):
        msg  = await ctx.send("🔍 Fetching meme...")
        meme = await fetch_meme(subreddit)
        if not meme:
            await msg.edit(content="❌ Couldn't fetch a meme right now. Try again!")
            return
        await msg.delete()
        await ctx.send(embed=make_meme_embed(meme))

    @bot.command(name="memetop")
    async def memetop(ctx, subreddit: str = "memes"):
        msg  = await ctx.send(f"🔍 Fetching from r/{subreddit}...")
        meme = await fetch_meme(subreddit)
        if not meme:
            await msg.edit(content="❌ Couldn't fetch a meme. Check the subreddit name.")
            return
        await msg.delete()
        await ctx.send(embed=make_meme_embed(meme))

    @tasks.loop(hours=AUTO_POST_INTERVAL)
    async def auto_meme():
        await bot.wait_until_ready()
        now = time.time()
        for guild in bot.guilds:
            # Prevent posting too soon after last post (reconnect guard)
            last = _last_auto_post.get(guild.id, 0)
            if now - last < MIN_POST_INTERVAL * 3600:
                print(f"[Memes] Skipping — posted too recently in {guild.name}")
                continue

            ch = discord.utils.find(
                lambda c: any(n in c.name.lower() for n in MEME_CHANNEL_NAMES),
                guild.text_channels,
            )
            if not ch:
                continue
            meme = await fetch_meme()
            if not meme:
                continue
            try:
                await ch.send(embed=make_meme_embed(meme))
                _last_auto_post[guild.id] = now
                print(f"[Memes] Auto posted in #{ch.name} in {guild.name}")
            except discord.Forbidden:
                print(f"[Memes] Missing permission in #{ch.name}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Memes] Auto post error: {e}")

    # Only start if not already running
    if not auto_meme.is_running():
        auto_meme.start()


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/summarize.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
from ai.conversation_engine import summarize_channel_messages

SUMMARY_LIMIT = 100


async def setup_summarize(bot: commands.Bot):

    @bot.command(name="summarize", aliases=["summary", "tldr"])
    async def summarize(ctx, channel: discord.TextChannel = None):
        target = channel or ctx.channel

        # Check bot has permission to read that channel
        perms = target.permissions_for(ctx.guild.me)
        if not perms.read_message_history:
            await ctx.send(f"❌ I don't have permission to read {target.mention}.")
            return

        msg = await ctx.send(f"📖 Reading last {SUMMARY_LIMIT} messages in {target.mention}...")

        # Fetch messages directly from Discord
        messages = []
        try:
            async for m in target.history(limit=SUMMARY_LIMIT):
                if m.author.bot:
                    continue
                if not m.content.strip():
                    continue
                messages.append(f"{m.author.display_name}: {m.content}")
            messages.reverse()
        except Exception as e:
            await msg.edit(content=f"❌ Could not read messages: {e}")
            return

        if not messages:
            await msg.edit(content=f"❌ No messages found in {target.mention}.")
            return

        await msg.edit(content=f"🧠 Summarising {len(messages)} messages...")

        summary = await summarize_channel_messages(messages)

        if not summary:
            await msg.edit(content="❌ Could not generate summary. Try again.")
            return

        embed = discord.Embed(
            title=f"📋 Summary of #{target.name}",
            description=summary,
            color=discord.Color.blurple(),
        )
        embed.set_footer(text=f"Based on last {len(messages)} messages • Requested by {ctx.author.display_name}")
        await msg.delete()
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/ask.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
from collections import defaultdict, deque
from ai.llm_client import call_llm
from database.memory_store import get_user, get_interests, get_top_topics

# ── Per-user conversation history (last 10 exchanges) ────────────────────────
# {user_id: deque([{"role": ..., "content": ...}, ...])}
_conversations: dict[int, deque] = defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=20))

MAX_HISTORY = 20   # 10 user + 10 assistant messages

SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are Jarvis, an intelligent AI assistant living inside a Discord server called {server_name}.

Server context:
- Members: {member_count}
- Active topics in this server: {topics}
- You are talking to: {username} (joined this server, XP level: {level})
- Their known interests: {interests}

Personality:
- Smart, helpful, and slightly witty
- You know this server and its members
- You remember what was said earlier in this conversation
- Keep responses concise but thorough — 2-5 sentences unless a detailed answer is needed
- Never say you are an AI or a bot unless directly asked
- Use the member's name naturally in conversation"""


async def build_system_prompt(guild: discord.Guild, member: discord.Member) -> str:
    # Get user data from database
    user_row  = await get_user(member.id)
    interests = await get_interests(member.id)
    topics    = await get_top_topics(5)

    level     = user_row[3] if user_row else 0
    xp        = user_row[2] if user_row else 0

    return SYSTEM_PROMPT.format(
        server_name  = guild.name,
        member_count = guild.member_count,
        topics       = ", ".join(topics) if topics else "general chat",
        username     = member.display_name,
        level        = f"{level} ({xp} XP)",
        interests    = ", ".join(interests) if interests else "not recorded yet",
    )


async def setup_ask(bot: commands.Bot):

    # ── !ask ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @bot.command(name="ask", aliases=["ai", "jarvis"])
    async def ask(ctx, *, question: str):
        user_id = ctx.author.id
        history = _conversations[user_id]

        async with ctx.typing():
            # Build system prompt with server + user context
            system = await build_system_prompt(ctx.guild, ctx.author)

            # Add user question to history
            history.append({"role": "user", "content": question})

            # Build full message list for API
            messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system}] + list(history)

            reply = await call_llm(messages, temperature=0.85, max_tokens=400)

        if not reply:
            await ctx.send("❌ Couldn't get a response right now. Try again.")
            history.pop()  # Remove the failed question from history
            return

        # Add assistant reply to history
        history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": reply})

        embed = discord.Embed(
            description=reply,
            color=discord.Color.blurple(),
        )
        embed.set_author(
            name=f"Jarvis — answering {ctx.author.display_name}",
            icon_url=bot.user.display_avatar.url,
        )
        embed.set_footer(text="Use !ask to continue the conversation • !resetchat to start fresh")
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)

    # ── !resetchat — clear conversation history ───────────────────────────────
    @bot.command(name="resetchat", aliases=["clearchat", "newchat"])
    async def resetchat(ctx):
        _conversations[ctx.author.id].clear()
        await ctx.send(f"🔄 {ctx.author.mention} Conversation history cleared. Fresh start!")

    # ── !whois — ask bot about a server member ────────────────────────────────
    @bot.command(name="whois")
    async def whois(ctx, member: discord.Member = None):
        target = member or ctx.author
        user_row  = await get_user(target.id)
        interests = await get_interests(target.id)

        xp    = user_row[2] if user_row else 0
        level = user_row[3] if user_row else 0
        msgs  = user_row[4] if user_row else 0

        embed = discord.Embed(
            title=f"👤 {target.display_name}",
            color=target.accent_color or discord.Color.blurple(),
        )
        embed.set_thumbnail(url=target.display_avatar.url)
        embed.add_field(name="XP",       value=str(xp),   inline=True)
        embed.add_field(name="Level",    value=str(level), inline=True)
        embed.add_field(name="Messages", value=str(msgs),  inline=True)
        embed.add_field(
            name="Interests",
            value=", ".join(interests) if interests else "None recorded yet",
            inline=False,
        )
        roles = [r.mention for r in target.roles if r.name != "@everyone"]
        if roles:
            embed.add_field(name="Roles", value=" ".join(roles[-5:]), inline=False)
        embed.set_footer(text=f"Joined Discord: {target.created_at.strftime('%b %Y')}")
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)


//===== TERMUXFILE: modules/birthday.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands, tasks
from datetime import datetime
import aiosqlite
import config

DB = config.DB_PATH
BIRTHDAY_ROLE_NAME = "🎂 Birthday"
BIRTHDAY_CHANNEL   = "birthdays"
ADMIN_USER_ID      = 0  # set to your Discord user ID to allow !setbday @member date


async def init_birthday_table():
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS birthdays (
                user_id   INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                username  TEXT,
                month     INTEGER,
                day       INTEGER
            )
        """)
        await db.commit()


async def save_birthday(user_id: int, username: str, month: int, day: int):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        await db.execute("""
            INSERT INTO birthdays (user_id, username, month, day)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET
                username=excluded.username,
                month=excluded.month,
                day=excluded.day
        """, (user_id, username, month, day))
        await db.commit()


async def get_birthday(user_id: int):
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute(
            "SELECT month, day FROM birthdays WHERE user_id=?", (user_id,)
        ) as cur:
            return await cur.fetchone()


async def get_todays_birthdays():
    today = datetime.utcnow()
    async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
        async with db.execute(
            "SELECT user_id, username FROM birthdays WHERE month=? AND day=?",
            (today.month, today.day)
        ) as cur:
            return await cur.fetchall()


def parse_date(date_str: str):
    for fmt in ("%d/%m", "%d-%m", "%m/%d", "%m-%d", "%B %d", "%b %d", "%d %B", "%d %b"):
        try:
            parsed = datetime.strptime(date_str.strip(), fmt)
            return parsed.month, parsed.day
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return None, None


async def setup_birthday(bot: commands.Bot):
    await init_birthday_table()

    # ── !setbday ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @bot.command(name="setbday", aliases=["setbirthday", "birthday"])
    async def setbday(ctx, *, date_str: str = None):
        # Check if admin is setting for another member
        # Usage: !setbday @member DD/MM  OR  !setbday DD/MM
        target = ctx.author
        actual_date = date_str

        if ctx.message.mentions:
            # Admin setting for someone else
            if not ctx.author.guild_permissions.administrator and str(ctx.author.id) != str(ADMIN_USER_ID):
                await ctx.send("❌ Only admins can set birthdays for other members.")
                return
            target = ctx.message.mentions[0]
            # Remove the mention from date string
            actual_date = date_str
            for mention in [f"<@{target.id}>", f"<@!{target.id}>"]:
                actual_date = actual_date.replace(mention, "").strip()

        if not actual_date:
            await ctx.send(
                "❌ Please provide a date.\n"
                "**Format:** `!setbday DD/MM` or `!setbday January 15`\n"
                "**Example:** `!setbday 15/03` or `!setbday March 15`"
            )
            return

        month, day = parse_date(actual_date)
        if not month or not day:
            await ctx.send(
                "❌ Could not parse that date.\n"
                "**Valid formats:** `15/03` · `15-03` · `March 15` · `15 March`"
            )
            return

        # Validate date
        try:
            datetime(2000, month, day)
        except ValueError:
            await ctx.send("❌ That date doesn't exist. Check the day and month.")
            return

        await save_birthday(target.id, str(target), month, day)

        month_name = datetime(2000, month, day).strftime("%B")
        if target == ctx.author:
            await ctx.send(
                f"🎂 Birthday saved! I'll wish you on **{month_name} {day}** every year. 🎉"
            )
        else:
            await ctx.send(
                f"🎂 Birthday set for {target.mention} — **{month_name} {day}**. 🎉"
            )

    # ── !mybday ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @bot.command(name="mybday", aliases=["mybirthday", "checkbday"])
    async def mybday(ctx, member: discord.Member = None):
        target = member or ctx.author
        row = await get_birthday(target.id)
        if not row:
            if target == ctx.author:
                await ctx.send("❌ You haven't set your birthday yet. Use `!setbday DD/MM`")
            else:
                await ctx.send(f"❌ {target.mention} hasn't set their birthday yet.")
            return
        month, day = row
        month_name = datetime(2000, month, day).strftime("%B")
        if target == ctx.author:
            await ctx.send(f"🎂 Your birthday is set to **{month_name} {day}**.")
        else:
            await ctx.send(f"🎂 {target.mention}'s birthday is **{month_name} {day}**.")

    # ── !removebday ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @bot.command(name="removebday", aliases=["deletebday"])
    async def removebday(ctx):
        async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
            await db.execute("DELETE FROM birthdays WHERE user_id=?", (ctx.author.id,))
            await db.commit()
        await ctx.send("🗑️ Your birthday has been removed.")

    # ── !upcomingbdays ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @bot.command(name="upcomingbdays", aliases=["birthdays", "bdays"])
    async def upcomingbdays(ctx):
        async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
            async with db.execute(
                "SELECT user_id, username, month, day FROM birthdays ORDER BY month, day"
            ) as cur:
                rows = await cur.fetchall()

        if not rows:
            await ctx.send("📭 No birthdays saved yet. Use `!setbday DD/MM` to add yours!")
            return

        today = datetime.utcnow()
        embed = discord.Embed(title="🎂 Server Birthdays", color=discord.Color.gold())

        lines = []
        for user_id, username, month, day in rows:
            member = ctx.guild.get_member(user_id)
            name   = member.mention if member else username
            month_name = datetime(2000, month, day).strftime("%b")

            # Check if birthday is today
            is_today = (month == today.month and day == today.day)
            prefix   = "🎉 **TODAY** → " if is_today else ""
            lines.append(f"{prefix}{name} — **{month_name} {day}**")

        embed.description = "\n".join(lines[:25])
        if len(rows) > 25:
            embed.set_footer(text=f"... and {len(rows) - 25} more")
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)

    # ── !testbday — test the birthday wish ────────────────────────────────────
    @bot.command(name="testbday")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def testbday(ctx, member: discord.Member = None):
        target = member or ctx.author
        await send_birthday_wish(bot, ctx.guild, target.id, str(target))
        await ctx.send(f"✅ Test birthday wish sent for {target.mention}.")

    # ── Daily birthday check ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    @tasks.loop(hours=1)
    async def birthday_check():
        await bot.wait_until_ready()
        now = datetime.utcnow()
        # Only run at midnight UTC
        if now.hour != 0:
            return
        rows = await get_todays_birthdays()
        for user_id, username in rows:
            for guild in bot.guilds:
                member = guild.get_member(user_id)
                if member:
                    await send_birthday_wish(bot, guild, user_id, username)

    birthday_check.start()


async def send_birthday_wish(bot, guild: discord.Guild, user_id: int, username: str):
    member = guild.get_member(user_id)
    if not member:
        return

    # Find or create birthday channel
    ch = discord.utils.find(
        lambda c: BIRTHDAY_CHANNEL in c.name.lower(),
        guild.text_channels,
    )
    if not ch:
        try:
            ch = await guild.create_text_channel(BIRTHDAY_CHANNEL)
            print(f"[Birthday] Created #{BIRTHDAY_CHANNEL} in {guild.name}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Birthday] Could not create birthday channel: {e}")
            return

    # Give birthday role
    role = discord.utils.get(guild.roles, name=BIRTHDAY_ROLE_NAME)
    if not role:
        try:
            role = await guild.create_role(
                name=BIRTHDAY_ROLE_NAME,
                color=discord.Color.gold(),
                mentionable=True,
            )
            print(f"[Birthday] Created role {BIRTHDAY_ROLE_NAME}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Birthday] Could not create birthday role: {e}")
            role = None

    if role:
        try:
            await member.add_roles(role)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Send birthday message
    embed = discord.Embed(
        title="🎂 Happy Birthday!",
        description=(
            f"🎉 Everyone wish {member.mention} a very **Happy Birthday!** 🎂🥳\n\n"
            f"Hope your day is filled with joy, good vibes, and all the things you love!\n\n"
            f"*From everyone in **{guild.name}*** 💛"
        ),
        color=discord.Color.gold(),
    )
    embed.set_thumbnail(url=member.display_avatar.url)
    embed.set_footer(text=f"🎈 {member.display_name}'s special day!")

    try:
        await ch.send(f"@everyone", embed=embed)
        print(f"[Birthday] Wished {member.display_name} in {guild.name}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Birthday] Could not send wish: {e}")
