//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/core.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
from modules.server_setup import setup_server
from modules.trivia_games import setup_trivia
from modules.summarize    import setup_summarize


class Core(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot = bot

    @commands.command(name="setup")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def cmd_setup(self, ctx):
        await ctx.send("⚙️ Setting up server, please wait...")
        await setup_server(ctx.guild)
        await ctx.send("✅ Server setup complete!")

    @commands.command(name="ping")
    async def ping(self, ctx):
        await ctx.send(f"🏓 Pong! `{round(self.bot.latency * 1000)}ms`")


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Core(bot))
    await setup_trivia(bot)
    await setup_summarize(bot)


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/leveling.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
import config
from database.memory_store import add_xp, get_xp, set_level

XP_THRESHOLDS = {5: 500, 10: 1200, 20: 3000}


class Leveling(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot = bot

    @commands.Cog.listener()
    async def on_message(self, message: discord.Message):
        if message.author.bot or not message.guild:
            return
        xp, current_level = await add_xp(
            message.author.id, str(message.author), config.XP_PER_MESSAGE
        )
        for lvl, threshold in sorted(XP_THRESHOLDS.items()):
            if xp >= threshold and current_level < lvl:
                await set_level(message.author.id, lvl)
                role_name = config.LEVEL_ROLES.get(lvl)
                role = discord.utils.get(message.guild.roles, name=role_name) if role_name else None
                if role:
                    try:
                        await message.author.add_roles(role)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                await message.channel.send(
                    f"🎉 {message.author.mention} reached **Level {lvl}**"
                    + (f" → **{role_name}**!" if role_name else "!")
                )
                break

    @commands.command(name="rank")
    async def rank(self, ctx, member: discord.Member = None):
        target = member or ctx.author
        xp, level = await get_xp(target.id)
        embed = discord.Embed(title=f"📊 {target.display_name}", color=discord.Color.gold())
        embed.add_field(name="XP",    value=str(xp),    inline=True)
        embed.add_field(name="Level", value=str(level), inline=True)
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Leveling(bot))


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/moderation.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
from collections import defaultdict, deque
import datetime
import time
import re
from ai.conversation_engine import check_toxicity
from database.memory_store import add_warning, get_warnings

EXEMPT_ROLES    = {"Admin", "Moderator", "MOD"}
MOD_ROLE_NAME   = "Moderator"
MOD_LOG_CHANNEL = "mod-log"
SPAM_LIMIT      = 5
SPAM_WINDOW     = 3.0
WARN_THRESHOLD  = 5

INVITE_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"(discord\.gg|discord\.com/invite|discordapp\.com/invite)/[\w-]+",
    re.IGNORECASE,
)

# ── Moderate blacklist — slurs and hate speech only ───────────────────────────
# Mild swearing is allowed. Only blocking racial slurs, homophobic,
# transphobic, religious hate, and targeted harassment terms.
BLACKLISTED_WORDS = [
    # Racial slurs
    "nigger", "nigga", "chink", "spic", "kike", "wetback", "gook",
    "coon", "porch monkey", "towelhead", "raghead", "beaner", "zipperhead",
    "cracker", "honky", "redneck slur", "jungle bunny", "sambo",
    # Homophobic slurs
    "faggot", "fag", "dyke", "tranny", "shemale", "homo slur",
    "queer slur", "sodomite",
    # Transphobic
    "tranny", "heshe", "shemale",
    # Religious hate
    "islamophobia slur", "christcuck", "kike",
    # Targeted harassment
    "kill yourself", "kys", "go die", "end yourself",
    "rope yourself", "drink bleach",
]

# Remove duplicates
BLACKLISTED_WORDS = list(set(BLACKLISTED_WORDS))


class Moderation(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot = bot
        self._spam_tracker: dict[int, deque] = defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=SPAM_LIMIT))

    def is_exempt(self, member: discord.Member) -> bool:
        return bool({r.name for r in member.roles} & EXEMPT_ROLES)

    async def get_mod_log(self, guild: discord.Guild):
        ch = discord.utils.find(
            lambda c: MOD_LOG_CHANNEL in c.name.lower(),
            guild.text_channels,
        )
        if not ch:
            try:
                overwrites = {
                    guild.default_role: discord.PermissionOverwrite(read_messages=False),
                }
                for role in guild.roles:
                    if role.name in EXEMPT_ROLES:
                        overwrites[role] = discord.PermissionOverwrite(read_messages=True)
                ch = await guild.create_text_channel(MOD_LOG_CHANNEL, overwrites=overwrites)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Moderation] Could not create mod-log: {e}")
        return ch

    async def log_action(self, guild, member, action, reason):
        ch = await self.get_mod_log(guild)
        if not ch:
            return
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title=f"🛡️ {action}",
            color=discord.Color.red(),
            timestamp=discord.utils.utcnow(),
        )
        embed.add_field(name="User",   value=f"{member} ({member.id})", inline=False)
        embed.add_field(name="Reason", value=reason,                    inline=False)
        embed.set_thumbnail(url=member.display_avatar.url)
        await ch.send(embed=embed)

    async def warn_member(self, message, reason, delete=True):
        member = message.author
        guild  = message.guild
        if delete:
            try:
                await message.delete()
            except Exception:
                pass
        await add_warning(member.id, reason)
        count = await get_warnings(member.id)
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title="⚠️ Warning",
            description=f"{member.mention} — **{reason}**",
            color=discord.Color.orange(),
        )
        embed.set_footer(text=f"Warning {count}/{WARN_THRESHOLD}")
        await message.channel.send(embed=embed, delete_after=10)
        await self.log_action(guild, member, "Warning", f"{reason} (total: {count})")
        if count >= WARN_THRESHOLD:
            mod_role = discord.utils.get(guild.roles, name=MOD_ROLE_NAME)
            mod_ch   = await self.get_mod_log(guild)
            if mod_ch:
                mention = mod_role.mention if mod_role else "@Moderator"
                await mod_ch.send(
                    f"{mention} ⚠️ **{member}** has reached **{count} warnings** "
                    f"and may need manual action (kick/ban)."
                )

    @commands.Cog.listener()
    async def on_message(self, message: discord.Message):
        if message.author.bot or not message.guild:
            return
        if self.is_exempt(message.author):
            return
        content = message.content
        now     = time.time()
        tracker = self._spam_tracker[message.author.id]
        tracker.append(now)
        if len(tracker) == SPAM_LIMIT:
            if now - tracker[0] < SPAM_WINDOW:
                await self.warn_member(message, "Spamming messages")
                tracker.clear()
                return
        if INVITE_PATTERN.search(content):
            await self.warn_member(message, "Posting Discord invite links")
            return
        if BLACKLISTED_WORDS:
            lower = content.lower()
            for word in BLACKLISTED_WORDS:
                if word.lower() in lower:
                    await self.warn_member(message, "Hate speech or slur used")
                    return
        if len(content) >= 5:
            result = await check_toxicity(content)
            if result.get("toxic"):
                await self.warn_member(message, result.get("reason", "Toxic content"))

    @commands.Cog.listener()
    async def on_message_edit(self, before, after):
        if after.author.bot or not after.guild:
            return
        if self.is_exempt(after.author):
            return
        if INVITE_PATTERN.search(after.content):
            await self.warn_member(after, "Posting Discord invite links")

    @commands.command(name="warn")
    @commands.has_permissions(manage_messages=True)
    async def warn_cmd(self, ctx, member: discord.Member, *, reason: str = "No reason given"):
        if self.is_exempt(member):
            await ctx.send("❌ Cannot warn exempt roles.")
            return
        await add_warning(member.id, reason)
        count = await get_warnings(member.id)
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title="⚠️ Manual Warning",
            description=f"{member.mention} warned by {ctx.author.mention}\n**Reason:** {reason}",
            color=discord.Color.orange(),
        )
        embed.set_footer(text=f"Total warnings: {count}")
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)
        await self.log_action(ctx.guild, member, "Manual Warning", f"{reason} by {ctx.author}")
        if count >= WARN_THRESHOLD:
            mod_role = discord.utils.get(ctx.guild.roles, name=MOD_ROLE_NAME)
            mod_ch   = await self.get_mod_log(ctx.guild)
            if mod_ch:
                mention = mod_role.mention if mod_role else "@Moderator"
                await mod_ch.send(f"{mention} ⚠️ **{member}** has reached **{count} warnings**.")

    @commands.command(name="warnings")
    @commands.has_permissions(manage_messages=True)
    async def warnings_cmd(self, ctx, member: discord.Member):
        count = await get_warnings(member.id)
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title=f"⚠️ Warnings — {member.display_name}",
            description=f"{member.mention} has **{count}** warning(s).",
            color=discord.Color.orange(),
        )
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)

    @commands.command(name="clearwarnings")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def clearwarnings(self, ctx, member: discord.Member):
        import aiosqlite, config
        async with aiosqlite.connect(config.DB_PATH) as db:
            await db.execute("DELETE FROM warnings WHERE user_id=?", (member.id,))
            await db.commit()
        await ctx.send(f"✅ Cleared all warnings for {member.mention}.")
        await self.log_action(ctx.guild, member, "Warnings Cleared", f"By {ctx.author}")

    @commands.command(name="timeout")
    @commands.has_permissions(moderate_members=True)
    async def timeout_cmd(self, ctx, member: discord.Member, minutes: int = 10, *, reason: str = "No reason given"):
        if self.is_exempt(member):
            await ctx.send("❌ Cannot timeout exempt roles.")
            return
        try:
            until = discord.utils.utcnow() + datetime.timedelta(minutes=minutes)
            await member.timeout(until, reason=reason)
            await ctx.send(f"🔇 {member.mention} timed out for **{minutes} min**. Reason: {reason}")
            await self.log_action(ctx.guild, member, f"Timeout ({minutes}min)", reason)
        except Exception as e:
            await ctx.send(f"❌ Could not timeout: {e}")

    @commands.command(name="purge")
    @commands.has_permissions(manage_messages=True)
    async def purge(self, ctx, amount: int = 10):
        if amount > 100:
            await ctx.send("❌ Max 100 messages at a time.")
            return
        deleted = await ctx.channel.purge(limit=amount + 1)
        await ctx.send(f"🗑️ Deleted **{len(deleted)-1}** messages.", delete_after=5)
        await self.log_action(
            ctx.guild, ctx.author, "Purge",
            f"{len(deleted)-1} messages in #{ctx.channel.name}"
        )

    @commands.command(name="addblacklist")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def addblacklist(self, ctx, *, word: str):
        word = word.lower().strip()
        if word not in BLACKLISTED_WORDS:
            BLACKLISTED_WORDS.append(word)
            await ctx.send(f"✅ Added `{word}` to blacklist.")
        else:
            await ctx.send(f"⚠️ Already blacklisted.")

    @commands.command(name="removeblacklist")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def removeblacklist(self, ctx, *, word: str):
        word = word.lower().strip()
        if word in BLACKLISTED_WORDS:
            BLACKLISTED_WORDS.remove(word)
            await ctx.send(f"✅ Removed `{word}` from blacklist.")
        else:
            await ctx.send(f"❌ Not in blacklist.")

    @commands.command(name="blacklist")
    @commands.has_permissions(manage_messages=True)
    async def show_blacklist(self, ctx):
        if not BLACKLISTED_WORDS:
            await ctx.send("📭 Blacklist is empty.")
            return
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title="🚫 Blacklisted Words",
            description="\n".join(f"• `{w}`" for w in sorted(BLACKLISTED_WORDS)),
            color=discord.Color.red(),
        )
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)

    @commands.command(name="modstats")
    @commands.has_permissions(manage_messages=True)
    async def modstats(self, ctx):
        import aiosqlite, config
        async with aiosqlite.connect(config.DB_PATH) as db:
            async with db.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM warnings") as cur:
                total = (await cur.fetchone())[0]
            async with db.execute(
                "SELECT user_id, COUNT(*) as c FROM warnings "
                "GROUP BY user_id ORDER BY c DESC LIMIT 5"
            ) as cur:
                top = await cur.fetchall()
        embed = discord.Embed(title="🛡️ Moderation Stats", color=discord.Color.red())
        embed.add_field(name="Total Warnings", value=str(total), inline=False)
        if top:
            lines = []
            for uid, count in top:
                m    = ctx.guild.get_member(uid)
                name = m.display_name if m else f"User {uid}"
                lines.append(f"**{name}** — {count} warnings")
            embed.add_field(name="Most Warned", value="\n".join(lines), inline=False)
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Moderation(bot))


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/activity.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands, tasks
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from collections import defaultdict, deque
import time
import random
import asyncio
import config
from ai.conversation_engine import (
    generate_contextual_reply,
    generate_conversation_starter,
    summarize_channel_messages,
)
from database.memory_store import (
    get_recent_messages,
    store_message,
    get_top_topics,
    get_channel_summary,
    save_channel_summary,
    count_channel_messages,
    get_summary_message_count,
    get_last_reply_time,
    set_last_reply_time,
)
from modules.personality_ai import handle_personality

ACTIVE_CHANNELS = ["cars", "music", "anime", "general"]


def is_active(name: str) -> bool:
    return any(ch in name.lower() for ch in ACTIVE_CHANNELS)


class Activity(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot           = bot
        self.msg_counter   = {}
        self.recent_topics = defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=10))
        self.inactivity_check.start()

    def cog_unload(self):
        self.inactivity_check.cancel()

    def record_starter(self, ch_id, starter):
        words = starter.strip().split()[:6]
        self.recent_topics[ch_id].append(" ".join(words))

    @commands.Cog.listener()
    async def on_message(self, message: discord.Message):
        if message.author.bot or not message.guild:
            return

        ch_id = message.channel.id
        await store_message(message.author.id, ch_id, message.content)
        self.msg_counter[ch_id] = self.msg_counter.get(ch_id, 0) + 1

        # Memory summariser
        total  = await count_channel_messages(ch_id)
        synced = await get_summary_message_count(ch_id)
        if total - synced >= config.SUMMARIZE_EVERY_N_MESSAGES:
            recent  = await get_recent_messages(ch_id, limit=config.SUMMARIZE_EVERY_N_MESSAGES)
            summary = await summarize_channel_messages(recent)
            if summary:
                await save_channel_summary(ch_id, summary, total)

        # Personality reply
        await handle_personality(self.bot, message)

        # Contextual AI reply
        if not is_active(message.channel.name):
            return
        if self.msg_counter[ch_id] % config.BOT_REPLY_EVERY_N_MESSAGES != 0:
            return
        if random.random() > config.AI_RESPONSE_PROBABILITY:
            return
        last_ts = await get_last_reply_time(ch_id)
        if time.time() - last_ts < config.AI_MIN_INTERVAL:
            return

        recent  = await get_recent_messages(ch_id, limit=8)
        summary = await get_channel_summary(ch_id)
        topics  = await get_top_topics(3)
        reply   = await generate_contextual_reply(recent, summary, topics)
        if reply:
            await asyncio.sleep(1.2)
            await message.channel.send(reply)
            await set_last_reply_time(ch_id, time.time())

    @tasks.loop(minutes=30)
    async def inactivity_check(self):
        await self.bot.wait_until_ready()
        for guild in self.bot.guilds:
            for ch_name in ACTIVE_CHANNELS:
                ch = discord.utils.find(
                    lambda c, n=ch_name: n in c.name.lower(),
                    guild.text_channels,
                )
                if not ch:
                    continue
                try:
                    last_msg = None
                    async for m in ch.history(limit=1):
                        last_msg = m
                    if not last_msg:
                        continue
                    delta = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - last_msg.created_at
                    if delta.total_seconds() > config.INACTIVITY_THRESHOLD_HOURS * 3600:
                        recent_topics = list(self.recent_topics[ch.id])
                        starter = await generate_conversation_starter(ch.name, recent_topics)
                        if starter:
                            self.record_starter(ch.id, starter)
                            embed = discord.Embed(
                                description=f"💬 {starter}",
                                color=discord.Color.teal(),
                            )
                            embed.set_footer(text="Jarvis | Keeping the chat alive 🤖")
                            await ch.send(embed=embed)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"[Activity] Error in {ch_name}: {e}")


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Activity(bot))


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/welcome.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
import time

WELCOME_COOLDOWN = 60


class Welcome(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot      = bot
        self.cooldown = {}

    def make_embed(self, member, guild):
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title=f"👋 Welcome to {guild.name}, {member.display_name}!",
            description=(
                f"Hey {member.mention}! Great to have you here. 🎉\n\n"
                f"📌 **Quick start:**\n"
                f"• Read the rules\n"
                f"• Verify yourself ✅\n"
                f"• Introduce yourself\n"
                f"• Grab your interest roles\n\n"
                f"Enjoy the community!"
            ),
            color=discord.Color.green(),
        )
        embed.set_thumbnail(url=member.display_avatar.url)
        embed.set_footer(text=f"Member #{guild.member_count}")
        return embed

    @commands.Cog.listener()
    async def on_member_join(self, member: discord.Member):
        key = (member.guild.id, member.id)
        now = time.time()
        if key in self.cooldown and now - self.cooldown[key] < WELCOME_COOLDOWN:
            return
        self.cooldown[key] = now
        ch = discord.utils.find(
            lambda c: "welcome" in c.name.lower(),
            member.guild.text_channels,
        )
        if not ch:
            return
        try:
            await ch.send(embed=self.make_embed(member, member.guild))
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Welcome] Error: {e}")

    @commands.command(name="testwelcome")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def testwelcome(self, ctx, member: discord.Member = None):
        target = member or ctx.author
        ch = discord.utils.find(
            lambda c: "welcome" in c.name.lower(),
            ctx.guild.text_channels,
        )
        if not ch:
            await ctx.send("❌ No welcome channel found.")
            return
        await ch.send(embed=self.make_embed(target, ctx.guild))
        await ctx.send(f"✅ Test welcome sent in {ch.mention}.")


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Welcome(bot))


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/reactions.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
import config
from database.memory_store import (
    save_reaction_role_message,
    get_reaction_role_messages,
    save_verify_message,
    get_verify_messages,
    save_hall_of_fame,
    is_in_hall_of_fame,
)


class Reactions(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot = bot

    @commands.Cog.listener()
    async def on_raw_reaction_add(self, payload: discord.RawReactionActionEvent):
        guild = self.bot.get_guild(payload.guild_id)
        if not guild:
            return
        member = guild.get_member(payload.user_id)
        if not member or member.bot:
            return
        emoji = str(payload.emoji)

        # Verify
        if emoji == "✅":
            rows = await get_verify_messages()
            if payload.message_id in [r[0] for r in rows]:
                role = discord.utils.get(guild.roles, name="Member")
                if role and role not in member.roles:
                    await member.add_roles(role)

        # Reaction roles
        if emoji in config.REACTION_ROLES:
            ids = await get_reaction_role_messages()
            if payload.message_id in ids:
                role = discord.utils.get(guild.roles, name=config.REACTION_ROLES[emoji])
                if role:
                    await member.add_roles(role)

        # Hall of fame
        channel = guild.get_channel(payload.channel_id)
        if channel:
            try:
                message = await channel.fetch_message(payload.message_id)
                total   = sum(r.count for r in message.reactions)
                if total >= config.HALL_OF_FAME_THRESHOLD:
                    if not await is_in_hall_of_fame(message.id):
                        hof = discord.utils.get(guild.text_channels, name="hall-of-fame")
                        if not hof:
                            hof = await guild.create_text_channel("hall-of-fame")
                        embed = discord.Embed(
                            description=message.content or "[Media]",
                            color=discord.Color.gold(),
                            timestamp=message.created_at,
                        )
                        embed.set_author(
                            name=message.author.display_name,
                            icon_url=message.author.display_avatar.url,
                        )
                        embed.add_field(name="Source", value=f"[Jump]({message.jump_url})")
                        embed.set_footer(text=f"⭐ {total} reactions | #{channel.name}")
                        if message.attachments:
                            embed.set_image(url=message.attachments[0].url)
                        await hof.send(embed=embed)
                        await save_hall_of_fame(
                            message.id, channel.id, message.author.id,
                            message.content or "", total,
                        )
            except Exception:
                pass

    @commands.Cog.listener()
    async def on_raw_reaction_remove(self, payload: discord.RawReactionActionEvent):
        emoji = str(payload.emoji)
        if emoji not in config.REACTION_ROLES:
            return
        ids = await get_reaction_role_messages()
        if payload.message_id not in ids:
            return
        guild = self.bot.get_guild(payload.guild_id)
        if not guild:
            return
        member = guild.get_member(payload.user_id)
        if not member or member.bot:
            return
        role = discord.utils.get(guild.roles, name=config.REACTION_ROLES[emoji])
        if role:
            await member.remove_roles(role)

    @commands.command(name="sendverify")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def sendverify(self, ctx):
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title="✅ Server Verification",
            description="React with ✅ to verify yourself and unlock the server!",
            color=discord.Color.blue(),
        )
        msg = await ctx.send(embed=embed)
        await msg.add_reaction("✅")
        await save_verify_message(msg.id, ctx.guild.id)

    @commands.command(name="sendreactionroles")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def sendreactionroles(self, ctx):
        lines = "\n".join(f"{e} → **{r}**" for e, r in config.REACTION_ROLES.items())
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title="🎭 Pick Your Roles",
            description=f"React below!\n\n{lines}",
            color=discord.Color.purple(),
        )
        msg = await ctx.send(embed=embed)
        for emoji in config.REACTION_ROLES:
            await msg.add_reaction(emoji)
        await save_reaction_role_message(msg.id, ctx.channel.id)


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Reactions(bot))


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/memes.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands, tasks
import httpx
import random
import time

MEME_SUBREDDITS   = ["memes", "dankmemes", "me_irl", "wholesomememes", "starterpacks"]
MEME_CHANNELS     = ["meme", "memes"]
POST_INTERVAL_HRS = 3
MIN_INTERVAL_HRS  = 2.5


class Memes(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot         = bot
        self.posted_urls = set()
        self.last_posted = {}
        self.auto_meme.start()

    def cog_unload(self):
        self.auto_meme.cancel()

    async def fetch(self, sub=None):
        sub     = sub or random.choice(MEME_SUBREDDITS)
        headers = {"User-Agent": "JarvisBot/1.0"}
        try:
            async with httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=15) as c:
                r = await c.get(
                    f"https://www.reddit.com/r/{sub}/hot.json?limit=50",
                    headers=headers,
                )
                r.raise_for_status()
                posts = r.json().get("data", {}).get("children", [])
            valid = [
                p["data"] for p in posts
                if not p["data"].get("is_video")
                and not p["data"].get("stickied")
                and p["data"].get("url", "").endswith((".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".gif"))
                and p["data"]["url"] not in self.posted_urls
            ]
            if not valid:
                return {}
            post = random.choice(valid)
            self.posted_urls.add(post["url"])
            if len(self.posted_urls) > 500:
                self.posted_urls.clear()
            return {
                "title":     post.get("title", "")[:256],
                "url":       post.get("url", ""),
                "subreddit": post.get("subreddit_name_prefixed", f"r/{sub}"),
                "score":     post.get("score", 0),
                "permalink": f"https://reddit.com{post.get('permalink', '')}",
            }
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Memes] Fetch error: {e}")
            return {}

    def make_embed(self, meme):
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title=meme.get("title", ""),
            url=meme.get("permalink", ""),
            color=discord.Color.orange(),
        )
        embed.set_image(url=meme["url"])
        embed.set_footer(text=f"{meme.get('subreddit','')} • 👍 {meme.get('score',0):,}")
        return embed

    @commands.command(name="meme")
    async def meme_cmd(self, ctx, sub: str = None):
        msg  = await ctx.send("🔍 Fetching meme...")
        meme = await self.fetch(sub)
        if not meme:
            await msg.edit(content="❌ Couldn't fetch a meme. Try again!")
            return
        await msg.delete()
        await ctx.send(embed=self.make_embed(meme))

    @commands.command(name="memetop")
    async def memetop(self, ctx, sub: str = "memes"):
        msg  = await ctx.send(f"🔍 Fetching from r/{sub}...")
        meme = await self.fetch(sub)
        if not meme:
            await msg.edit(content="❌ Couldn't fetch. Check subreddit name.")
            return
        await msg.delete()
        await ctx.send(embed=self.make_embed(meme))

    @tasks.loop(hours=POST_INTERVAL_HRS)
    async def auto_meme(self):
        await self.bot.wait_until_ready()
        now = time.time()
        for guild in self.bot.guilds:
            last = self.last_posted.get(guild.id, 0)
            if now - last < MIN_INTERVAL_HRS * 3600:
                continue
            ch = discord.utils.find(
                lambda c: any(n in c.name.lower() for n in MEME_CHANNELS),
                guild.text_channels,
            )
            if not ch:
                continue
            meme = await self.fetch()
            if not meme:
                continue
            try:
                await ch.send(embed=self.make_embed(meme))
                self.last_posted[guild.id] = now
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Memes] Post error: {e}")


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Memes(bot))


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/ask.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
from collections import defaultdict, deque
from ai.llm_client import call_llm
from database.memory_store import get_user, get_interests, get_top_topics

SYSTEM = """You are Jarvis, an intelligent AI assistant in a Discord server called {server}.
You are talking to {name} (Level {level}, {xp} XP).
Their interests: {interests}
Server active topics: {topics}
Be helpful, concise, and slightly witty. Remember earlier messages in this conversation."""


class Ask(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot           = bot
        self.conversations = defaultdict(lambda: deque(maxlen=20))

    @commands.command(name="ask", aliases=["ai", "jarvis"])
    async def ask(self, ctx, *, question: str):
        uid     = ctx.author.id
        history = self.conversations[uid]
        async with ctx.typing():
            row       = await get_user(uid)
            interests = await get_interests(uid)
            topics    = await get_top_topics(5)
            system    = SYSTEM.format(
                server    = ctx.guild.name,
                name      = ctx.author.display_name,
                level     = row[3] if row else 0,
                xp        = row[2] if row else 0,
                interests = ", ".join(interests) or "not set",
                topics    = ", ".join(topics) or "general",
            )
            history.append({"role": "user", "content": question})
            messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system}] + list(history)
            reply    = await call_llm(messages, temperature=0.85, max_tokens=400)
        if not reply:
            history.pop()
            await ctx.send("❌ No response. Try again.")
            return
        history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": reply})
        embed = discord.Embed(description=reply, color=discord.Color.blurple())
        embed.set_author(
            name=f"Jarvis — {ctx.author.display_name}",
            icon_url=self.bot.user.display_avatar.url,
        )
        embed.set_footer(text="!ask to continue • !resetchat to start fresh")
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)

    @commands.command(name="resetchat", aliases=["clearchat"])
    async def resetchat(self, ctx):
        self.conversations[ctx.author.id].clear()
        await ctx.send(f"🔄 {ctx.author.mention} Conversation cleared!")

    @commands.command(name="whois")
    async def whois(self, ctx, member: discord.Member = None):
        target    = member or ctx.author
        row       = await get_user(target.id)
        interests = await get_interests(target.id)
        embed     = discord.Embed(
            title=f"👤 {target.display_name}",
            color=discord.Color.blurple(),
        )
        embed.set_thumbnail(url=target.display_avatar.url)
        embed.add_field(name="XP",       value=str(row[2] if row else 0), inline=True)
        embed.add_field(name="Level",    value=str(row[3] if row else 0), inline=True)
        embed.add_field(name="Messages", value=str(row[4] if row else 0), inline=True)
        embed.add_field(
            name="Interests",
            value=", ".join(interests) if interests else "None yet",
            inline=False,
        )
        roles = [r.mention for r in target.roles if r.name != "@everyone"]
        if roles:
            embed.add_field(name="Roles", value=" ".join(roles[-5:]), inline=False)
        embed.set_footer(text=f"Joined Discord: {target.created_at.strftime('%b %Y')}")
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Ask(bot))


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/birthday.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands, tasks
from datetime import datetime
import aiosqlite
import config

DB               = config.DB_PATH
BIRTHDAY_ROLE    = "🎂 Birthday"
BIRTHDAY_CHANNEL = "birthdays"


class Birthday(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot = bot
        self.daily_check.start()

    def cog_unload(self):
        self.daily_check.cancel()

    async def cog_load(self):
        async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
            await db.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS birthdays (
                    user_id  INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    username TEXT,
                    month    INTEGER,
                    day      INTEGER
                )
            """)
            await db.commit()

    def parse_date(self, s):
        for fmt in ("%d/%m", "%d-%m", "%m/%d", "%B %d", "%b %d", "%d %B", "%d %b"):
            try:
                p = datetime.strptime(s.strip(), fmt)
                return p.month, p.day
            except ValueError:
                continue
        return None, None

    @commands.command(name="setbday", aliases=["setbirthday"])
    async def setbday(self, ctx, *, args: str):
        target      = ctx.author
        date_string = args
        if ctx.message.mentions:
            if not ctx.author.guild_permissions.administrator:
                await ctx.send("❌ Only admins can set birthdays for others.")
                return
            target = ctx.message.mentions[0]
            for m in [f"<@{target.id}>", f"<@!{target.id}>"]:
                date_string = date_string.replace(m, "").strip()
        month, day = self.parse_date(date_string)
        if not month:
            await ctx.send("❌ Invalid date. Use: `15/03` or `March 15`")
            return
        try:
            datetime(2000, month, day)
        except ValueError:
            await ctx.send("❌ That date doesn't exist.")
            return
        async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
            await db.execute("""
                INSERT INTO birthdays (user_id, username, month, day)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET
                    username=excluded.username,
                    month=excluded.month,
                    day=excluded.day
            """, (target.id, str(target), month, day))
            await db.commit()
        mn = datetime(2000, month, day).strftime("%B")
        if target == ctx.author:
            await ctx.send(f"🎂 Birthday saved! I'll wish you on **{mn} {day}** every year!")
        else:
            await ctx.send(f"🎂 Set {target.mention}'s birthday to **{mn} {day}**!")

    @commands.command(name="mybday")
    async def mybday(self, ctx, member: discord.Member = None):
        target = member or ctx.author
        async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
            async with db.execute(
                "SELECT month, day FROM birthdays WHERE user_id=?", (target.id,)
            ) as cur:
                row = await cur.fetchone()
        if not row:
            await ctx.send("❌ No birthday set. Use `!setbday DD/MM`")
            return
        mn = datetime(2000, row[0], row[1]).strftime("%B")
        await ctx.send(f"🎂 {target.mention}'s birthday: **{mn} {row[1]}**")

    @commands.command(name="upcomingbdays", aliases=["bdays"])
    async def upcomingbdays(self, ctx):
        async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
            async with db.execute(
                "SELECT user_id, username, month, day FROM birthdays ORDER BY month, day"
            ) as cur:
                rows = await cur.fetchall()
        if not rows:
            await ctx.send("📭 No birthdays saved yet!")
            return
        today = datetime.utcnow()
        embed = discord.Embed(title="🎂 Server Birthdays", color=discord.Color.gold())
        lines = []
        for uid, uname, month, day in rows:
            m      = ctx.guild.get_member(uid)
            name   = m.mention if m else uname
            mn     = datetime(2000, month, day).strftime("%b")
            prefix = "🎉 **TODAY** → " if (month == today.month and day == today.day) else ""
            lines.append(f"{prefix}{name} — **{mn} {day}**")
        embed.description = "\n".join(lines[:25])
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)

    @commands.command(name="testbday")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def testbday(self, ctx, member: discord.Member = None):
        target = member or ctx.author
        await self.wish(ctx.guild, target.id, str(target))
        await ctx.send(f"✅ Test birthday sent for {target.mention}.")

    async def wish(self, guild, uid, uname):
        member = guild.get_member(uid)
        if not member:
            return
        ch = discord.utils.find(
            lambda c: BIRTHDAY_CHANNEL in c.name.lower(),
            guild.text_channels,
        )
        if not ch:
            try:
                ch = await guild.create_text_channel(BIRTHDAY_CHANNEL)
            except Exception:
                return
        role = discord.utils.get(guild.roles, name=BIRTHDAY_ROLE)
        if not role:
            try:
                role = await guild.create_role(
                    name=BIRTHDAY_ROLE,
                    color=discord.Color.gold(),
                    mentionable=True,
                )
            except Exception:
                role = None
        if role:
            try:
                await member.add_roles(role)
            except Exception:
                pass
        embed = discord.Embed(
            title="🎂 Happy Birthday!",
            description=(
                f"🎉 Everyone wish {member.mention} a **Happy Birthday!** 🥳\n\n"
                f"Hope your day is amazing!\n\n*From everyone in **{guild.name}*** 💛"
            ),
            color=discord.Color.gold(),
        )
        embed.set_thumbnail(url=member.display_avatar.url)
        try:
            await ch.send("@everyone", embed=embed)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Birthday] Error: {e}")

    @tasks.loop(hours=1)
    async def daily_check(self):
        await self.bot.wait_until_ready()
        now = datetime.utcnow()
        if now.hour != 0:
            return
        async with aiosqlite.connect(DB) as db:
            async with db.execute(
                "SELECT user_id, username FROM birthdays WHERE month=? AND day=?",
                (now.month, now.day),
            ) as cur:
                rows = await cur.fetchall()
        for uid, uname in rows:
            for guild in self.bot.guilds:
                await self.wish(guild, uid, uname)


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Birthday(bot))


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/fun.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
import config


class Fun(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot = bot

    @commands.command(name="personas")
    async def personas(self, ctx):
        embed = discord.Embed(title="🎭 AI Personalities", color=discord.Color.magenta())
        for name, data in config.BOT_PERSONALITIES.items():
            embed.add_field(
                name=f"{data['emoji']} {name}",
                value=data["style"][:100] + "...",
                inline=False,
            )
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)

    @commands.command(name="summon")
    @commands.has_permissions(manage_messages=True)
    async def summon(self, ctx, name: str):
        from ai.conversation_engine import generate_personality_reply
        from database.memory_store  import get_recent_messages, get_channel_summary
        data = config.BOT_PERSONALITIES.get(name)
        if not data:
            names = ", ".join(config.BOT_PERSONALITIES.keys())
            await ctx.send(f"❌ Unknown personality. Choose: {names}")
            return
        recent  = await get_recent_messages(ctx.channel.id, limit=6)
        summary = await get_channel_summary(ctx.channel.id)
        reply   = await generate_personality_reply(name, data["style"], recent, summary)
        if reply:
            await ctx.send(f"{data['emoji']} **{name}:** {reply}")
        else:
            await ctx.send("❌ No response. Try again.")

    @commands.command(name="ragebait")
    @commands.has_permissions(administrator=True)
    async def ragebait(self, ctx, level: int = None):
        """Set ragebait mode level. 0 = off, 1 = mild, 2 = counter-argues, 3 = chaos.
        Usage: !ragebait 2   or   !ragebait 0  (to disable)"""
        if level is None:
            current = config.RAGEBAIT_LEVEL
            status  = f"Level {current}" if current > 0 else "OFF"
            labels  = {0: "off", 1: "🌶️ mild hot takes", 2: "🔥 counter-argues", 3: "💀 full chaos"}
            await ctx.send(
                f"🔥 **Ragebait Mode** is currently **{status}** ({labels.get(current, '?')})\n"
                f"Use `!ragebait <0-3>` to change."
            )
            return

        if level not in (0, 1, 2, 3):
            await ctx.send("❌ Level must be 0 (off), 1, 2, or 3.")
            return

        config.RAGEBAIT_LEVEL = level
        labels = {
            0: "❌ Ragebait mode **disabled**.",
            1: "🌶️ Ragebait level **1** — mild hot takes. Gentle chaos.",
            2: "🔥 Ragebait level **2** — counter-argues everything. Strap in.",
            3: "💀 Ragebait level **3** — full chaos agent. God help this server.",
        }
        await ctx.send(labels[level])

    @commands.command(name="help")
    async def help_cmd(self, ctx):
        embed = discord.Embed(title="📖 Bot Commands", color=discord.Color.blurple())
        cmds = [
            ("!ask <question>",       "Ask Jarvis anything"),
            ("!whois [@member]",      "Show member info"),
            ("!rank [@member]",       "Show XP and level"),
            ("!meme",                 "Get a random meme"),
            ("!trivia",               "Start a trivia game"),
            ("!summarize [#channel]", "Summarise chat"),
            ("!setbday DD/MM",        "Set your birthday"),
            ("!upcomingbdays",        "See all birthdays"),
            ("!summon <name>",        "Summon an AI personality"),
            ("!personas",             "List AI personalities"),
            ("!resetchat",            "Clear AI chat history"),
            ("!ragebait [0-3]",       "Set ragebait level (admin)"),
            ("!sendverify",           "Post verify panel (admin)"),
            ("!sendreactionroles",    "Post role picker (admin)"),
            ("!setup",                "Setup server (admin)"),
            ("!testwelcome",          "Test welcome (admin)"),
        ]
        for cmd, desc in cmds:
            embed.add_field(name=cmd, value=desc, inline=False)
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Fun(bot))


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/polls.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
import asyncio
import re

OPTION_EMOJIS = ["1️⃣", "2️⃣", "3️⃣", "4️⃣", "5️⃣", "6️⃣", "7️⃣", "8️⃣", "9️⃣", "🔟"]
YES_EMOJI     = "✅"
NO_EMOJI      = "❌"
POLL_CHANNEL  = "polls"

_active_polls: dict = {}


def clean_name(name: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9]", "", name).lower()


async def get_or_create_poll_channel(guild: discord.Guild) -> discord.TextChannel:
    # Find existing polls channel
    ch = discord.utils.find(
        lambda c: "poll" in c.name.lower(),
        guild.text_channels,
    )
    if ch:
        print(f"[Polls] Found existing channel: #{ch.name}")
        return ch

    # Find COMMUNITY category by stripping symbols
    category = discord.utils.find(
        lambda c: "community" in clean_name(c.name),
        guild.categories,
    )
    print(f"[Polls] Category found: {category.name if category else 'None'}")

    try:
        ch = await guild.create_text_channel(
            POLL_CHANNEL,
            category=category,
            topic="Community polls — vote with reactions!",
        )
        print(f"[Polls] Created #polls successfully in {guild.name}")
        return ch
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Polls] Failed to create #polls: {e}")
        return None


class Poll(commands.Cog):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot = bot
        print("[Polls] Cog loaded successfully")

    @commands.command(name="poll")
    @commands.has_any_role("Moderator", "MOD", "Admin")
    async def poll(self, ctx, *, args: str):
        print(f"[Polls] !poll called by {ctx.author} with args: {args}")

        # Parse time limit
        duration_seconds = None
        time_match = re.search(r"(\d+)(m|h)\s*$", args, re.IGNORECASE)
        if time_match:
            amount           = int(time_match.group(1))
            unit             = time_match.group(2).lower()
            duration_seconds = amount * 60 if unit == "m" else amount * 3600
            args             = args[:time_match.start()].strip()

        # Split question and options
        if "?" in args:
            parts       = args.split("?", 1)
            question    = parts[0].strip() + "?"
            options_raw = parts[1].strip()
        else:
            question    = args.strip()
            options_raw = ""

        options  = [o.strip() for o in options_raw.split(",") if o.strip()] if options_raw else []
        is_yesno = len(options) == 0

        print(f"[Polls] Question: {question} | Options: {options} | YesNo: {is_yesno}")

        if len(options) > 10:
            await ctx.send("❌ Max 10 options allowed.", delete_after=5)
            return
        if len(options) == 1:
            await ctx.send("❌ Need at least 2 options or leave empty for Yes/No.", delete_after=5)
            return

        poll_channel = await get_or_create_poll_channel(ctx.guild)
        if not poll_channel:
            await ctx.send("❌ Could not create #polls channel. Make sure I have Manage Channels permission.")
            return

        embed = discord.Embed(title="📊 " + question, color=discord.Color.blurple())
        embed.set_author(
            name=f"Poll by {ctx.author.display_name}",
            icon_url=ctx.author.display_avatar.url,
        )

        if is_yesno:
            embed.description = f"{YES_EMOJI} Yes\n{NO_EMOJI} No"
            emojis_to_add     = [YES_EMOJI, NO_EMOJI]
        else:
            embed.description = "\n".join(
                f"{OPTION_EMOJIS[i]} {opt}" for i, opt in enumerate(options)
            )
            emojis_to_add = OPTION_EMOJIS[:len(options)]

        if duration_seconds:
            mins     = duration_seconds // 60
            hrs      = mins // 60
            time_str = f"{hrs}h" if hrs else f"{mins}m"
            embed.set_footer(text=f"⏱️ Closes in {time_str} • React to vote!")
        else:
            embed.set_footer(text="React to vote! No time limit.")

        try:
            await ctx.message.delete()
        except Exception:
            pass

        if ctx.channel.id != poll_channel.id:
            await ctx.send(f"📊 Poll posted in {poll_channel.mention}!", delete_after=5)

        print(f"[Polls] Sending poll to #{poll_channel.name}")
        poll_msg = await poll_channel.send(embed=embed)

        for emoji in emojis_to_add:
            await poll_msg.add_reaction(emoji)

        _active_polls[poll_msg.id] = {
            "question":   question,
            "options":    options,
            "is_yesno":   is_yesno,
            "emojis":     emojis_to_add,
            "channel_id": poll_channel.id,
            "author":     ctx.author.display_name,
            "message_id": poll_msg.id,
        }

        print(f"[Polls] Poll created successfully: {question}")

        if duration_seconds:
            self.bot.loop.create_task(
                self.close_poll(poll_msg, duration_seconds, poll_channel)
            )

    @poll.error
    async def poll_error(self, ctx, error):
        print(f"[Polls] ERROR: {error}")
        if isinstance(error, commands.MissingAnyRole):
            await ctx.send("❌ Only Moderators and Admins can create polls.", delete_after=8)
        elif isinstance(error, commands.MissingRequiredArgument):
            await ctx.send("❌ Usage: `!poll Question? option1, option2` or `!poll Yes/No question?`", delete_after=8)
        else:
            await ctx.send(f"❌ Poll error: `{error}`", delete_after=15)

    async def close_poll(self, poll_msg, duration, channel):
        await asyncio.sleep(duration)
        poll_data = _active_polls.get(poll_msg.id)
        if not poll_data:
            return
        try:
            poll_msg = await channel.fetch_message(poll_msg.id)
        except Exception:
            return

        results = {}
        for reaction in poll_msg.reactions:
            emoji = str(reaction.emoji)
            if emoji in poll_data["emojis"]:
                results[emoji] = reaction.count - 1

        if not results or sum(results.values()) == 0:
            await channel.send("📊 Poll ended — no votes were cast.")
            _active_polls.pop(poll_msg.id, None)
            return

        embed = discord.Embed(
            title=f"📊 Poll Results: {poll_data['question']}",
            color=discord.Color.green(),
        )

        if poll_data["is_yesno"]:
            yes   = results.get(YES_EMOJI, 0)
            no    = results.get(NO_EMOJI, 0)
            total = yes + no
            embed.description = (
                f"{YES_EMOJI} Yes — **{yes}** votes\n"
                f"{NO_EMOJI} No — **{no}** votes\n\n"
                f"Total: **{total}**"
            )
            winner = "✅ Yes" if yes > no else ("❌ No" if no > yes else "It's a tie!")
            embed.add_field(name="🏆 Result", value=winner, inline=False)
        else:
            options = poll_data["options"]
            emojis  = poll_data["emojis"]
            total   = sum(results.values())
            lines   = []
            for i, (emoji, opt) in enumerate(zip(emojis, options)):
                votes   = results.get(emoji, 0)
                percent = round((votes / total * 100) if total > 0 else 0)
                bar     = "█" * (percent // 10) + "░" * (10 - percent // 10)
                lines.append(f"{emoji} **{opt}**\n`{bar}` {votes} votes ({percent}%)")
            embed.description = "\n\n".join(lines)

            max_votes = max(results.values())
            winners   = [e for e, v in results.items() if v == max_votes]
            if len(winners) == 1:
                winner_idx = emojis.index(winners[0])
                embed.add_field(
                    name="🏆 Winner",
                    value=f"{winners[0]} **{options[winner_idx]}** with **{max_votes}** votes!",
                    inline=False,
                )
            else:
                tie_names = ", ".join(options[emojis.index(w)] for w in winners)
                embed.add_field(name="🏆 Result", value=f"Tie: **{tie_names}**", inline=False)

        embed.set_footer(text=f"Poll by {poll_data['author']} • Total: {sum(results.values())} votes")
        await channel.send(embed=embed)
        _active_polls.pop(poll_msg.id, None)

    @commands.command(name="endpoll")
    @commands.has_any_role("Moderator", "MOD", "Admin")
    async def endpoll(self, ctx, message_id: int = None):
        poll_channel  = await get_or_create_poll_channel(ctx.guild)
        channel_polls = [
            (mid, data) for mid, data in _active_polls.items()
            if data["channel_id"] == (poll_channel.id if poll_channel else ctx.channel.id)
        ]
        if not channel_polls:
            await ctx.send("❌ No active polls.", delete_after=5)
            return
        mid       = message_id or channel_polls[-1][0]
        poll_data = _active_polls.get(mid)
        if not poll_data:
            await ctx.send("❌ Poll not found.", delete_after=5)
            return
        try:
            poll_msg = await poll_channel.fetch_message(mid)
        except Exception:
            await ctx.send("❌ Could not find poll message.", delete_after=5)
            return
        await self.close_poll(poll_msg, 0, poll_channel)
        await ctx.send("✅ Poll ended.", delete_after=5)

    @commands.command(name="polls")
    async def list_polls(self, ctx):
        if not _active_polls:
            await ctx.send("📭 No active polls right now.")
            return
        embed = discord.Embed(title="📊 Active Polls", color=discord.Color.blurple())
        for data in _active_polls.values():
            embed.add_field(name=data["question"], value=f"By {data['author']}", inline=False)
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Poll(bot))


//===== TERMUXFILE: cogs/movies.py =====
import discord
from discord.ext import commands
from modules.movie_night import setup_movie_night


class Movies(commands.Cog):
    """Movie Night + Yandex movie search commands."""

    def __init__(self, bot):
        self.bot = bot

    @commands.command(name="moviehelp")
    async def movie_help(self, ctx):
        embed = discord.Embed(title="🎬 Movie Commands", color=discord.Color.red())
        embed.add_field(
            name="!movienight <title>",
            value=(
                "Post a **Movie Night** announcement embed.\n"
                "Pings `@movies` role & shows the VC link.\n"
                "*(requires Manage Events)*"
            ),
            inline=False,
        )
        embed.add_field(
            name="!moviesearch <title>",
            value=(
                "Search Yandex for download/stream links.\n"
                "Results are **DM'd** to keep the channel clean."
            ),
            inline=False,
        )
        await ctx.send(embed=embed)


async def setup(bot):
    await bot.add_cog(Movies(bot))
    await setup_movie_night(bot)
